					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[Meet the Generation17 Young Leaders: The Story of Soumya Dabriwal]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/meet-the-generation17-young-leaders-the-story-of-soumya-dabriwal</link>
				<pubDate>Fri, 04 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02152309/Mobile-Generation17-Global-Goals-Soumya-Dabriwal_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Generation17]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Global Goals]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[UNDP]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Young Leaders]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4iqvXk7</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Soumya Dabriwal is a member of Generation17, a Samsung and United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) partnership empowering young people who are contributing to the Global Goals. Since 2020, the initiative has supported Young Leaders worldwide with Samsung Galaxy technology, mentorship, and networking opportunities to amplify their stories and solutions. Soumya Dabriwal was 21, volunteering as […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Soumya Dabriwal is a member of <a href="https://www.undp.org/generation17" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Generation17</a>, a Samsung and <a href="https://www.undp.org/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">United Nations Development Programme (UNDP)</a> partnership empowering young people who are contributing to the <a href="https://sdgs.un.org/goals" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Global Goals</a>.</p>



<p>Since 2020, the initiative has supported Young Leaders worldwide with Samsung Galaxy technology, mentorship, and networking opportunities to amplify their stories and solutions.</p>



<p>Soumya Dabriwal was 21, volunteering as a math teacher in villages in India and Ghana, when she noticed a pattern she couldn’t explain. Girls were absent from class far more often than boys — even siblings from the same household, where the brother would show up while his sister stayed home.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio"><div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">
<iframe loading="lazy" title="Meet the Generation17 Young Leaders: The Story of Soumya Dabriwal | Samsung" width="500" height="281" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/UAgKjWnCm84?feature=oembed" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allowfullscreen></iframe> 
</div></figure>



<p>When Soumya asked, no one would answer why. The reality hit her when students warned her not to drink water in the heat because the school had no toilets. It quickly became clear: without basic facilities, girls had no choice but to skip school during their periods.</p>



<p>She began researching and found that around the world, girls lacked <a href="https://www.who.int/news/item/28-05-2024-global-report-reveals-major-gaps-in-menstrual-health-and-hygiene-in-schools" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">health education, support, and resources</a> to stay in school and achieve their dreams. “I was extremely frustrated and disappointed. It felt unfair that biology was restricting girls from attaining their full potential,” Soumya says. “Instead of waiting for someone else to take action, I became obsessed with wanting to solve the problem.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Tech-Enabled, Women-Led</strong></h2>



<p>In 2017, she co-founded Project <a href="https://baala.in/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Baala</a>, which means “young girl” in Hindi, a social enterprise addressing menstrual hygiene and reproductive health access through innovative solutions. The mission is to advance the UN Global Goals for quality education (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/4-quality-education/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 4</a>), good health and well-being (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/3-good-health-and-well-being/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 3</a>), gender equality (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/5-gender-equality/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 5</a>), and decent work and economic growth (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/8-decent-work-and-economic-growth/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 8</a>).</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02143826/Samsung-Mobile-Generation17-Global-Goals-Soumya-Dabriwal_Main1.jpg" alt="Community workshops are at the heart of Baala's approach, combining education with open conversations that help break long-standing taboos." class="wp-image-177002" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Community workshops are at the heart of Baala’s approach, combining education with open conversations that help break long-standing taboos.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Soumya began leading workshops where girls learned about their bodies and health. But education alone wasn’t enough. Without access to affordable, sustainable feminine hygiene products, girls were still missing school. Since nothing on the market met their needs, Soumya created a prototype using materials she found at home, turning a simple idea into a solution that today reaches millions of girls.</p>



<p>Next, she and her growing team began training local women to deliver health education and feminine hygiene products. Known as Baala Bosses, these women earn an income, often for the first time, while helping support their families and gaining confidence as community leaders.</p>



<p>Technology has played a critical role in Baala’s growth. Social media connects the organization with women and girls around the world, while online training enables Baala Bosses to learn and share knowledge wherever they are. Digital field data helps the team measure its impact and continuously strengthen its programs. “Technology has really helped us scale and reach people we wouldn’t have been able to otherwise,” says Soumya.</p>



<p>Over the past eight years, Baala has trained more than 4,500 Baala Bosses, reached 1.8 million women and girls, engaged more than 10,000 men to help break the stigma around women’s health and distributed 5.1 million reusable period products through more than 260 partnerships with grassroots organizations.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02144054/Samsung-Mobile-Generation17-Global-Goals-Soumya-Dabriwal_Main2.jpg" alt="Soumya and the Baala team meet at their Delhi office to review insights from the field and plan future initiatives." class="wp-image-177003" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Soumya and the Baala team meet at their Delhi office to review insights from the field and plan future initiatives.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Building a Movement for Women’s Health</strong></h2>



<p>Building Baala wasn’t easy. Soumya recalls obstacles at every level, from convincing people to take a young female founder seriously to confronting long-held cultural taboos. Those experiences only reinforced her belief that lasting change had to come from within communities themselves.</p>



<p>Today, Baala Bosses are leading change in their own neighborhoods by educating families, creating opportunities for others and inspiring the next generation. “Women who once kept their faces covered now walk with their hair flying, speaking openly about this topic,” Soumya says. “Some become community leaders. Some become entrepreneurs. Some become health mobilizers. Some become teachers.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02144417/Samsung-Mobile-Generation17-Global-Goals-Soumya-Dabriwal_Main3.jpg" alt="Participants gather for a photo following one of Baala's community workshops in northern India." class="wp-image-177004" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Participants gather for a photo following one of Baala’s community workshops in northern India.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Advancing Women’s Health, Rights, and Opportunities</strong></h2>



<p>Soumya, now 30, dreams of continuing to expand Baala’s impact. By 2030, she hopes to reach 10 million women and girls around the world through product innovation, expanded education, and technology-enabled solutions.</p>



<p>To make that vision a reality, her team launched the Baala Action Lab, an online platform that partners with universities to advance women’s health worldwide. The initiative combines academic research with real-world evidence to inform better policies and investments in women’s health.</p>



<p>Soumya says the ultimate measure of success will be the day Baala is no longer needed — when women and girls have the knowledge, opportunities, and support they need to thrive on their own.</p>



<p>Until then, she keeps showing up.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it is expanding its flat design, previously available on select premium models, to its new 24-inch front-load washer lineup. The new models are currently on display at Samsung Connect, Samsung’s exhibition being held during IFA 2026 from Sept. 2-6, offering visitors an early look ahead of the official launch.<sup data-fn="a1c59f5b-c0e9-4bda-85a9-9390021daee4" class="fn"><a href="#a1c59f5b-c0e9-4bda-85a9-9390021daee4" id="a1c59f5b-c0e9-4bda-85a9-9390021daee4-link">1</a></sup></p>



<p>“Customers around the world increasingly expect home appliances to harmonize with their living environments,” said Hyoung Min Park, Vice President and Head of the Customer Experience Team of the Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “By bringing our flat design to a wider range of laundry appliances, Samsung aims to deliver a consistent design experience that combines refined aesthetics with thoughtful usability.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Refined Flat Design Improves Interior Fit and Usability</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung’s expansion of its signature flat design to the new 24-inch front-load washer lineup marks the latest evolution of the company’s design approach across its home appliance portfolio.</p>



<p>Compared to Samsung’s previous designs that featured more protruding front elements, the updated design offers a flat front surface with a more uniform appearance. This creates a cleaner, more sophisticated look that blends naturally into modern interiors and living environments.</p>



<p>Beyond aesthetics, the design also enhances everyday usability. The larger door with a wider opening angle enables easier access when loading and unloading laundry, making the task more convenient. More broadly, the new lineup and the showcase during IFA reflect Samsung’s continued efforts to expand refined design experiences to more consumers and living spaces.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Features Designed for Everyday Laundry Needs</strong></h2>



<p>In addition to the redesigned exterior, the new lineup incorporates key laundry technologies previously available on select premium models, bringing advanced washing experiences to more consumers. To address growing consumer demand for faster wash cycles and more efficient laundry routines, the lineup features Bubble Shot<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />, which sprays water throughout the drum. This helps reduce wash time while maintaining effective cleaning performance,<sup data-fn="894c893e-6417-4d58-9554-f09f9c04e598" class="fn"><a href="#894c893e-6417-4d58-9554-f09f9c04e598" id="894c893e-6417-4d58-9554-f09f9c04e598-link">2</a></sup> providing a more convenient laundry experience for users.</p>



<p>The lineup also comes with Ecobubble<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> technology, which allows detergent to dissolve more quickly and distribute more evenly across fabrics, helping users save energy while washing clothes effectively, even at low temperatures.<sup data-fn="f56c2702-838f-43d3-a650-fecabab953ac" class="fn"><a href="#f56c2702-838f-43d3-a650-fecabab953ac" id="f56c2702-838f-43d3-a650-fecabab953ac-link">3</a></sup></p>



<p>By combining a refined exterior with advanced laundry technologies, Samsung’s new 24-inch washer lineup delivers a more intuitive laundry experience across diverse households. The company will continue to expand user-focused design approaches and technologies across its home appliance portfolio.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Exhibition Information</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung is showcasing its latest innovations at Samsung Connect, which is being held at Humboldt Carré in Berlin from Sept. 2-6. The new 24-inch washer lineup is on display, allowing visitors to see the products’ refreshed design ahead of their official launch.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03073807/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-IFA-2026-New-24-Inch-Washer-Lineup-Samsung-Connect_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Connect at Humboldt Carré in Berlin" class="wp-image-177035" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung Connect at Humboldt Carré in Berlin</figcaption></figure>

<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="a1c59f5b-c0e9-4bda-85a9-9390021daee4">The products shown at IFA 2026 are development-stage display models. Specifications are subject to change, and commercial availability has not been determined. <a href="#a1c59f5b-c0e9-4bda-85a9-9390021daee4-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="894c893e-6417-4d58-9554-f09f9c04e598">Based on internal testing using the Cotton cycle on a WW7400D model with Bubble Shot<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> compared to a conventional Samsung washing machine without Bubble Shot<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />. 																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>For Lefa Makgato, CSR Manager at Samsung Electronics Africa, empowering the next generation of digital innovators is more than a mission — it’s the driving force behind her work leading Samsung’s CSR initiatives across Africa.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28171631/Samsung-Mobile-Voices-of-Galaxy-Lefa-Makgato_Main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics Africa Office CSR Manager, Lefa Makgato" class="wp-image-176888" /></figure>



<p>Growing up in a small township in South Africa, Lefa Makgato didn’t know what to dream about because technology felt so far out of reach. But in 2012, a single encounter with Samsung’s Solar Powered Internet School changed everything.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="281" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28171807/Samsung-Mobile-Voices-of-Galaxy-Lefa-Makgato_Main2.jpg" alt="An image of Solve for Tomorrow " class="wp-image-176889" /></figure>



<p>Since joining Samsung, Lefa has dedicated herself to giving back the same opportunity she once received to other students. Today, she leads Samsung’s CSR initiatives across Africa, including Solve for Tomorrow, which empowers young students in Africa to become the next generation of digital innovators. She’s not just teaching students to use technology — she’s equipping them to shape Africa’s future.</p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Redefines Laundry With New A-70% and 13 Kg Bespoke AI Washers at IFA 2026]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-redefines-laundry-with-new-a-70-and-13-kg-bespoke-ai-washers-at-ifa-2026</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 02 Sep 2026 15:30:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02151321/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-IFA-2026-New-A-70-and-13-kg-Bespoke-AI-Washers_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />

						<category><![CDATA[Home Appliances]]></category>

Samsung Electronics today announced that it will showcase two new Bespoke AI Washer models during IFA 2026: a 10 kg model designed to deliver A-70% energy efficiency and a 13 kg large-capacity model engineered to fit within a standard 600 mm depth. As […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02151409/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-IFA-2026-New-A-70-and-13-kg-Bespoke-AI-Washers_main1.jpg" alt="New A-70% and 13 Kg Bespoke AI Washers" class="wp-image-177017" /></figure>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading">*For illustrative purposes only. Product design and specifications are subject to change.</h4>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it will showcase two new Bespoke AI Washer models during IFA 2026:<sup data-fn="1d67a5bb-cb07-44d3-9c4c-bed98d0a48ef" class="fn"><a href="#1d67a5bb-cb07-44d3-9c4c-bed98d0a48ef" id="1d67a5bb-cb07-44d3-9c4c-bed98d0a48ef-link">1</a></sup> a 10 kg model designed to deliver A-70% energy efficiency<sup data-fn="f22a7c1b-00a7-4334-ab00-92fa5dfda701" class="fn"><a href="#f22a7c1b-00a7-4334-ab00-92fa5dfda701" id="f22a7c1b-00a7-4334-ab00-92fa5dfda701-link">2</a></sup> and a 13 kg large-capacity model engineered to fit within a standard 600 mm depth.</p>



<p>As the official main sponsor of the IFA Creator Hub, Samsung will showcase its latest innovations at Humboldt Carré in Berlin, Germany from Sept. 2-6. The new Bespoke AI Washers will be among the products on display, highlighting Samsung’s latest advances in technology.</p>



<p>The two new models combine improved energy efficiency and greater capacity with space-efficient design and AI-powered fabric care to help consumers manage laundry more efficiently and conveniently.</p>



<p>“At Samsung, we are continually challenging the traditional trade-offs between energy efficiency, capacity and convenience,” said Hyoung Min Park, VP and Head of the Customer Experience Team of the Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “During IFA 2026, we’re demonstrating how innovation can help consumers save energy, handle larger loads and enjoy a more intelligent and convenient laundry experience.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Larger 10 Kg Bespoke AI Washer Brings New Efficiency Milestone</strong></h2>



<p>The Bespoke AI Washer with A-70% energy efficiency marks a new step forward for Samsung, following the A-55% Bespoke AI Washer introduced at IFA 2024 and the A-65% model showcased at IFA 2025.</p>



<p>The new washer will be designed to consume up to 70% less energy than the minimum energy efficiency requirement for a Class A rating in Europe, while increasing washing capacity from 9 kg<sup data-fn="5dc667b5-829b-4c28-b7d7-e24f05b26023" class="fn"><a href="#5dc667b5-829b-4c28-b7d7-e24f05b26023" id="5dc667b5-829b-4c28-b7d7-e24f05b26023-link">3</a></sup> to 10 kg. By improving capacity and energy efficiency simultaneously, the new model builds on the company’s continued research into overcoming the traditional compromise between washing larger loads and reducing energy consumption.</p>



<p>Samsung has achieved these gains through engineering improvements across key areas, including motor efficiency, thermal management, and wash-cycle design. Together, these technologies reduce electricity consumption while maintaining effective cleaning performance and everyday usability.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>13 Kg Bespoke AI Washer Offers Increased Capacity With Standard-Depth Footprint</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung will also showcase the new Bespoke AI Washer with large capacity, which brings a 13 kg washing capacity to a standard European design depth of 600 mm.</p>



<p>Using SpaceMax<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> technology, which creates more space inside to enhance the mechanical design and incorporate a larger drum without increasing the external dimensions, the washer increases internal volume while maintaining a compact 24-inch form factor. This space-efficient design allows users to wash larger loads, bedding, and bulkier items — while helping the appliance fit within common European kitchen, utility-room, and furniture configurations.</p>



<p>The washer also incorporates Samsung’s mono-body design, creating a unified form designed to blend naturally into a wide range of interiors. A slim front frame reinforces its streamlined silhouette, while refined colors, materials, and finishes contribute to a clean, understated appearance.</p>



<p>At the center of the interface is a large touch display visually integrated with the detergent compartment. From this display, users can intuitively access cycle controls, connected features, and personalized laundry services through SmartThings.<sup data-fn="583ca707-2bc9-462c-9ace-b7eeb1ec606a" class="fn"><a href="#583ca707-2bc9-462c-9ace-b7eeb1ec606a" id="583ca707-2bc9-462c-9ace-b7eeb1ec606a-link">4</a></sup></p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Auto Open Door Helps Keep the Washer Interior Fresh</strong></h2>



<p>For added convenience, both washers feature Auto Open Door. Once a wash cycle is complete, the washer door opens gradually to help release residual moisture from inside the drum. By automatically ventilating the interior after a cycle, Auto Open Door helps keep the washer interior fresh between cycles.</p>



<p>Together, the new A-70% and 13 kg Bespoke AI Washers reflect Samsung’s continued effort to challenge traditional laundry trade-offs. They deliver great capacity with lower energy consumption, making everyday laundry more intelligent and convenient.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="1d67a5bb-cb07-44d3-9c4c-bed98d0a48ef">The Bespoke AI Washer models shown at IFA 2026 are development-stage display models. Specifications are subject to change, and commercial availability to be determined. <a href="#1d67a5bb-cb07-44d3-9c4c-bed98d0a48ef-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="f22a7c1b-00a7-4334-ab00-92fa5dfda701">Based on Samsung internal testing. The energy consumption of this Bespoke AI Washer [WF90*/24 (10kg)] achieves an EEI of 15.5 (15.3 kWh / 100 cycles), which is up to 70.2% more energy efficient compared to the minimum threshold for Class A (EEI 52 for 10 kg models) under Regulation (EU) No 2019/2014. Final testing to be conducted prior to EPREL registration. Results may vary depending on the usage environment. Energy ratings tested with the Eco 40–60 program. <a href="#f22a7c1b-00a7-4334-ab00-92fa5dfda701-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="5dc667b5-829b-4c28-b7d7-e24f05b26023">Compared to the previous model WF90H09C4S with A-65% energy efficiency and 9 kg washing capacity. <a href="#5dc667b5-829b-4c28-b7d7-e24f05b26023-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 3"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="583ca707-2bc9-462c-9ace-b7eeb1ec606a">Available on Android and iOS devices. 									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced the opening of “Design is an Act of Love” at Design Miami Seoul 2026, a global design event taking place in Seoul from Sept. 1-6. The exhibition celebrates a human-centered design vision rooted in a deep understanding of and care for people. 																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced the opening of <strong>“Design is an Act of Love”</strong> at Design Miami Seoul 2026, a global design event taking place in Seoul from Sept. 1-6.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/01092344/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Press-Release_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics today announced the opening of “Design is an Act of Love” at Design Miami Seoul 2026, a global design event taking place in Seoul from Sept. 1-6." class="wp-image-176981" /></figure>



<p>The exhibition celebrates a human-centered design vision rooted in a deep understanding of and care for people. Visitors will experience Samsung’s Expressive Design through works by 14 Korean artists reinterpreting a selection of Samsung products, which are being presented on the 2026 OLED TV S95H along with 14 custom frames that become part of the artworks themselves. Together, the works showcase Samsung’s Expressive Design through a range of individual perspectives and interpretations.</p>



<p>“‘Design is an Act of Love’ represents how we approach design — with a deep understanding of people at its core,” said Mauro Porcini, President and Chief Design Officer of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics. “Creativity starts with people, and AI can help amplify our imagination and take our ideas further. At Samsung, we will continue to expand possibilities for people through a more human, diverse, and expressive approach to design.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Reinterpreting Samsung Products: 14 Korean Artists’ Artworks on OLED TV S95H With 14 Custom Frames</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/01092411/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Press-Release_main2.jpg" alt="Each of the 14 artists who collaborated with Samsung drew on their unique perspective and imagination to develop their concepts, while using AI as a tool to expand their ideas and creative expression." class="wp-image-176982" /></figure>



<p>Each of the 14 artists who collaborated with Samsung drew on their unique perspective and imagination to develop their concepts, while using AI as a tool to expand their ideas and creative expression. Each work began with the artist’s own creativity, with AI helping bring those ideas to life in different ways.</p>



<p>A range of Samsung products were reinterpreted by the artists, including Music Studio 5, Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hub, and Galaxy Z Fold 8, spanning eight products across mobile devices, home appliances, and TVs. The result is a collection of expressive artworks that embody Samsung’s design principles as well as the artists’ individual aesthetics.</p>



<p>Samsung is also showcasing the S95H OLED TV with 14 custom frames, demonstrating how a single product can reflect diverse perspectives through the company’s Expressive Design strategy.</p>



<p>The S95H features Samsung’s Float Layer Design, which makes the screen appear to float above a real-metal plate. Its structure and materials become part of the design, giving the TV a distinctive presence while allowing it to blend naturally into its surroundings.</p>



<p>For the exhibition, the custom frames bring different perspectives to the S95H, corresponding with the artwork displayed on screen and showing how the same product can take on a distinct character depending on the space and individual preferences.</p>



<p>The exhibition will take place from Sept. 1-6 at Dongdaemun Design Plaza (DDP) in Seoul. On Sept. 3, Chief Design Officer Porcini will take part in a discussion to share his perspectives on Samsung’s design vision.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-large"><img loading="lazy" width="819" height="1024" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/01094635/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Press-Release_main3-819x1024.jpg" alt="The exhibition will take place from Sept. 1-6 at Dongdaemun Design Plaza (DDP) in Seoul. On Sept. 3, Chief Design Officer Porcini will take part in a discussion to share his perspectives on Samsung’s design vision." class="wp-image-176990" /></figure>
]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[[Interview] The People Behind the Galaxy Z Fold8 Series’ Camera Innovations ①: How My FanCam Keeps Favorite People in Focus]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/interview-the-people-behind-the-galaxy-z-fold8-series-camera-innovations-%e2%91%a0-how-my-fancam-keeps-favorite-people-in-focus</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 01 Sep 2026 09:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31160827/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Technology]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Interview]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[My FanCam]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4gtiKFK</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Unveiled at Galaxy Unpacked July 2026, Samsung Electronics’ Galaxy Z Fold8 series introduces My FanCam — a new feature that lets users focus their edits on a person of their choice — as well as an enhanced Portrait Video that recreates the look of professional camera lenses. While the two features offer different camera experiences, both […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<blockquote class="wp-block-quote is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p>Unveiled at Galaxy Unpacked July 2026, Samsung Electronics’ Galaxy Z Fold8 series introduces My FanCam — a new feature that lets users focus their edits on a person of their choice — as well as an enhanced Portrait Video that recreates the look of professional camera lenses.<br><br>While the two features offer different camera experiences, both draw on expertise spanning foundational technology research, feature development, and user experience design. <br><br>As part of “The People Behind the Galaxy Z Fold8 Series’ Camera Innovations,” Samsung Newsroom sat down with experts from Samsung Research and the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business to discuss the technical challenges and collaboration behind the two features.</p>
</blockquote>



<p>Since the launch of the Galaxy Z Fold8 series, one feature has been generating buzz among K-pop fans as a must-have — My FanCam. Users simply select a person in a video, and My FanCam automatically tracks their movements throughout.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31164205/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main1.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ My FanCam on Galaxy Z Fold8</figcaption></figure>



<p>The feature makes it easy to put someone in the spotlight, from a favorite artist in concert footage to family and friends in everyday moments.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31161841/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main2.jpg" alt="(From left) Sohee Hong, Sunghwan Kim, and Hwangpil Park" class="wp-image-176931" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Sherry Hong, Sunghwan Kim, and Hwangpil Park</figcaption></figure>



<p>Behind this experience is Samsung Research’s AI technology for reliably tracking people in video, integrated into a mobile feature through close collaboration with the MX Business. Samsung Newsroom spoke with Hwangpil Park of the Visual Technology Team at Samsung Research as well as Sunghwan Kim and Sherry Hong of the Multimedia Development Group at the MX Business about My FanCam.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A User-Centered Approach</strong></h2>



<p><strong>Q. What inspired My FanCam?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Hong:</strong> We started by asking how we could make it easier for users to create videos focused on the people they want to see. As shooting, editing, and sharing content on smartphones have become commonplace, fan cams of favorite artists have gained popularity on social media. In response to this trend, we envisioned My FanCam — a feature that lets anyone create a video centered on a person of their choice with just a few taps — and worked with Samsung Research to bring the idea to life.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>The Technology Behind Reliable Tracking</strong></h2>



<p><strong>Q. What technology enables My FanCam to continuously track someone in a video?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Park:</strong> The key to My FanCam’s ability to follow a selected person even in complex scenes is an AI model that precisely recognizes individuals. The model identifies and remembers each person’s location and visual characteristics, allowing it to recognize the same person throughout the video and continue tracking them.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="425" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31190506/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main3_F.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-176967" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ My FanCam’s process</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. What was the main focus for the user experience?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> We focused on reducing wait times while editing. Users may want to switch between people in group videos, but reanalyzing the video each time would mean waiting again. We designed My FanCam to analyze multiple people at once, letting users switch between subjects and instantly see the results once the initial analysis is complete.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31164911/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main4.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Users can switch between people after the initial video analysis.</figcaption></figure>



<p>We also simplified the editing process. Users can easily adjust the aspect ratio to fit the scene and create a video that suits their needs.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="304" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31162404/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main5.jpg" alt="My FanCam’s aspect ratio adjustment feature" class="wp-image-176934" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ My FanCam’s aspect ratio adjustment feature</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. How did the two teams collaborate to turn the technology into a Galaxy feature?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Hong:</strong> It was crucial not only to identify the issues, but also to find a solution for the said issues; validating that the results were key to our process. The more the concept was solidified, the more full-fledged the development process became. In order to keep up with the development progress, weekly meetings turned into daily meetings and in those daily meetings, we made sure to track progress every morning.</p>



<p>Testing a large range of scenarios is important before rolling out new features. To help the development team test with a wide range of scenarios, multiple team members helped with the process. Some brought in old videos from the college days, such as dance club videos. Others would bring a phone and film new footage, such as climbing hobbies.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31162835/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main6.jpg" alt="Hong shows a personal video used for testing." class="wp-image-176935" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Hong shows a personal video used for testing.</figcaption></figure>



<p><strong>Park:</strong> Technology that worked well in a research environment sometimes performed differently with real-world footage due to variables such as fast movement and people overlapping in the frame. When the MX Business shared these cases, Samsung Research analyzed the causes and refined the AI model and tracking methods. We then validated the changes in the product environment, repeating the process to improve the feature.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31162930/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main7.jpg" alt="Park discusses the development of My FanCam." class="wp-image-176936" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Park discusses the development of My FanCam.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. What was the biggest challenge in bringing My FanCam to mobile devices?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> The key challenge was processing AI workloads entirely on-device without a separate server. Longer videos require more computation, so we had to consider processing speed, power consumption, and heat generation. Samsung Research optimized the AI model to achieve the required results with less computation, while the MX Business streamlined the entire process — from video decoding to data transfer and analysis — reducing computational load with minimal impact on performance.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31163045/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main8.jpg" alt="Kim explains how My FanCam was optimized for on-device performance." class="wp-image-176937" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Kim explains how My FanCam was optimized for on-device performance.</figcaption></figure>



<p><strong>Park:</strong> We also had to account for situations that can make it difficult to select a person in videos. At school performances or sports days, for example, the person users want to focus on may not appear until midway through the video or may be obscured by others. We therefore designed My FanCam so users can find a moment when their subject is clearly visible and select them at any point in the video.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>From Favorite Artists to Everyday Moments</strong></h2>



<p><strong>Q. Any tips for getting the most out of My FanCam?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> When filming, I recommend using a high-resolution setting and framing the shot a little wider to leave space around the person. When selecting a subject, choose a frame where they don’t overlap too much with others and their face, upper body, or full body is clearly visible.</p>



<p>My FanCam also works with existing footage, such as videos of past concerts, trips, or children’s activities. Because users don’t need to follow a particular person while filming, they can focus more on the moment itself.</p>



<p>For example, users can set up a tripod to film a workout, then use My FanCam to create a video centered on themselves without someone else behind the camera.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="700" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31163241/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main9.jpg" alt="My FanCam in different scenarios" class="wp-image-176938" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ My FanCam in different scenarios</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. How do you hope users will use My FanCam?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Park:</strong> Since My FanCam works with videos not shot on a Galaxy device, I hope people will try it with a wide range of footage. It can offer a fresh perspective on old videos, helping users revisit meaningful moments by focusing on someone special.</p>



<p><strong>Hong:</strong> Some may think that being a fan is only about liking an idol or celebrity. But to me, it simply means caring deeply about someone or something — whether that’s an artist, family member, friend, or even yourself. I’d like to see people use My FanCam to capture the people and moments that matter to them.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31163343/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main10.jpg" alt="Developers discuss how collaboration made My FanCam possible." class="wp-image-176940" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Developers discuss how collaboration made My FanCam possible.</figcaption></figure>



<p>What began as an idea focused on user convenience has evolved into My FanCam, a new video experience combining AI, mobile optimization, and close teamwork. From concerts to everyday life, My FanCam makes it easier to capture and edit videos of moments worth remembering.</p>



<p>In part two of this series<strong>, </strong>Samsung Newsroom will explore how the large-aperture camera lens emulation technology brings the expressive qualities of professional lenses to mobile devices and the collaboration behind its development.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

<enclosure url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/31164911/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-series-My-FanCam-Interview_main4.mp4" length="534" type="video/mp4" />

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Expands the Galaxy Book6 Lineup, Making Galaxy Book More Accessible]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-expands-the-galaxy-book6-lineup-making-galaxy-book-more-accessible</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 01 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28155012/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced a new 14-inch Galaxy Book6, expanding the Galaxy Book6 lineup with a more accessible model designed for everyday computing. Starting at $799, Galaxy Book6 combines up to 25 hours of battery life, Intel® Core™ 3 and Core™ 5 performance, Galaxy AI-enabled features, and the design language of the Galaxy Book6 family. […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced a new 14-inch Galaxy Book6, expanding the Galaxy Book6 lineup with a more accessible model designed for everyday computing. Starting at $799, Galaxy Book6 combines up to 25 hours of battery life, Intel® Core<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> 3 and Core<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> 5 performance, Galaxy AI-enabled features, and the design language of the Galaxy Book6 family<strong>.</strong></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28160735/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_main1.jpg" alt="Galaxy Book6, expanding the lineup with a more accessible model built for everyday use" class="wp-image-176857" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Book6, expanding the lineup with a more accessible model built for everyday use </figcaption></figure>



<p>“With the new Galaxy Book6, we wanted to make the Galaxy Book experience available for more people,” said Mincheol Lee, EVP, Head of Galaxy Ecosystems Business Team at Samsung Electronics. “At a more accessible price, it brings together the everyday essentials users value, including dependable performance, long-lasting battery life, connected Galaxy experiences, and Galaxy Book6’s design DNA, giving users confidence that lasts beyond the purchase.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Performance and Battery for Everyday Use</strong></h2>



<p>The new Galaxy Book6 is designed around the tasks people rely on every day, from browsing the web and creating documents to video calls, multitasking, and streaming content. With up to 25 hours of battery life<sup data-fn="9ec8ee11-12f4-427d-a755-087ed536a975" class="fn"><a href="#9ec8ee11-12f4-427d-a755-087ed536a975" id="9ec8ee11-12f4-427d-a755-087ed536a975-link">1</a></sup> on a single charge, users can work, study, and enjoy content for longer throughout the day. When it is time to power up, 45W fast charging provides approximately 33% charge in 30 minutes.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-large"><img loading="lazy" width="1024" height="576" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28161317/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_main2-1024x576.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-176858" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Up to 25 hours of battery life on a single charge, with 45W fast charging to quickly power back up when needed</figcaption></figure>



<p>That long-lasting power is paired with Intel® Core<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> Series 3 processor performance, bringing Intel’s latest architecture and power efficiency to essential PC experiences. The processor also supports Galaxy AI<sup data-fn="f7483872-8b04-498e-9d73-44df181429a0" class="fn"><a href="#f7483872-8b04-498e-9d73-44df181429a0" id="f7483872-8b04-498e-9d73-44df181429a0-link">2</a></sup> and other AI features designed to help users work, create, and get things done more easily.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Built With Galaxy Book6 Design DNA</strong></h2>



<p>The latest addition to the Galaxy Book6 lineup carries forward the design language of the series, defined by clean, balanced lines and a symmetrical design. A centered logo further reinforces a unified Galaxy Book6 identity.</p>



<p>At 15.7mm thin and 1.35kg, Galaxy Book6 combines a slim profile with portability that makes it easy to carry throughout the day, from home and school to work and travel. For the first time on Galaxy Book, Violet Silver brings a fresh expression to the lineup, adding a distinctive touch to the clean, understated Galaxy Book6 design. A classic Gray option is also available.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28161510/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_main3.jpg" alt="For the first time on Galaxy Book, Violet Silver brings a fresh, distinctive look to the clean, understated Galaxy Book6 design, alongside a classic Gray option." class="wp-image-176859" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ For the first time on Galaxy Book, Violet Silver brings a fresh, distinctive look to the clean, understated Galaxy Book6 design, alongside a classic Gray option.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The 16:10 display provides more vertical viewing space for documents, web pages, and multitasking, while anti-reflective technology minimizes glare for clearer, more comfortable viewing wherever you are. Galaxy Book6 also keeps commonly used connections close at hand.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28161534/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_main4.jpg" alt="An anti-glare finish helps reduce reflections for clearer, more comfortable viewing wherever you are." class="wp-image-176860" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ An anti-glare finish helps reduce reflections for clearer, more comfortable viewing wherever you are.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Its two USB Type-C<sup data-fn="816f0d2e-5c80-4d60-ba85-d6318a9e2d93" class="fn"><a href="#816f0d2e-5c80-4d60-ba85-d6318a9e2d93" id="816f0d2e-5c80-4d60-ba85-d6318a9e2d93-link">3</a></sup> ports offer the same functionality, so users can connect with less guesswork, while USB Type-A,<sup data-fn="dc56781e-c255-4fb9-950a-e8e482574475" class="fn"><a href="#dc56781e-c255-4fb9-950a-e8e482574475" id="dc56781e-c255-4fb9-950a-e8e482574475-link">4</a></sup> HDMI 1.4,<sup data-fn="a3bda3ae-b3ea-4bc9-89f0-f3ef7779571c" class="fn"><a href="#a3bda3ae-b3ea-4bc9-89f0-f3ef7779571c" id="a3bda3ae-b3ea-4bc9-89f0-f3ef7779571c-link">5</a></sup> and a 3.5mm headphone jack, give users a versatile range of connectivity for displays, peripherals, and accessories without relying on additional dongles.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="611" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28161617/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_main5.jpg" alt="Two USB Type-C ports with the same functionality, making everyday connections simpler and more intuitive, plus USB Type-A, HDMI 1.4, and a 3.5mm headphone jack" class="wp-image-176861" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Two USB Type-C ports with the same functionality, making everyday connections simpler and more intuitive, plus USB Type-A, HDMI 1.4, and a 3.5mm headphone jack</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Experiences That Work Better Together</strong></h2>



<p>The Galaxy Book6 brings together Galaxy AI and connected experiences across Galaxy devices to make it easier to find information, move content and work across screens.</p>



<p><strong>AI Select</strong><sup data-fn="a5beed14-4b41-4d50-8b9e-ec6b411455af" class="fn"><a href="#a5beed14-4b41-4d50-8b9e-ec6b411455af" id="a5beed14-4b41-4d50-8b9e-ec6b411455af-link">6</a></sup> lets users select content directly on the screen and instantly access helpful information and insights while browsing, shopping, or viewing content online. It also includes <strong>AI cut out</strong>, which lets users instantly remove image backgrounds to prepare polished visuals for presentations, e-commerce listings, or marketing materials. <strong>Note Assist</strong><sup data-fn="4bbe6a75-fcaa-49a9-a441-fb565e730a8e" class="fn"><a href="#4bbe6a75-fcaa-49a9-a441-fb565e730a8e" id="4bbe6a75-fcaa-49a9-a441-fb565e730a8e-link">7</a></sup> summarizes notes and surfaces key information so users can review important details faster.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28161855/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_main6_F.jpg" alt="AI Select lets users quickly access helpful information and use AI Cut Out to refine visuals, making everyday tasks easier." class="wp-image-176863" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ AI Select lets users quickly access helpful information and use AI Cut Out to refine visuals, making everyday tasks easier.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Content also moves more easily across Galaxy devices. <strong>Quick Share</strong> enables fast file sharing between compatible devices, while <strong>Storage Share</strong> lets users access photos, videos, and files across compatible Galaxy smartphones and Galaxy Book without transferring them first — making it easier to bring content from a phone directly into a document, presentation, or creative project.</p>



<p>Galaxy Book6 also helps users work naturally across screens. <strong>Multi Control</strong><sup data-fn="2a269b86-e565-4d18-84c0-671731db1a24" class="fn"><a href="#2a269b86-e565-4d18-84c0-671731db1a24" id="2a269b86-e565-4d18-84c0-671731db1a24-link">8</a></sup> allows a single keyboard and mouse to control multiple supported Galaxy devices, while <strong>Second Screen</strong><sup data-fn="774da205-b312-40c3-8d17-135fec2285e0" class="fn"><a href="#774da205-b312-40c3-8d17-135fec2285e0" id="774da205-b312-40c3-8d17-135fec2285e0-link">9</a></sup> turns a Galaxy Tab into an additional display. <strong>Nearby Devices</strong><sup data-fn="c38e74dc-bc49-482d-a200-9a163da4dac5" class="fn"><a href="#c38e74dc-bc49-482d-a200-9a163da4dac5" id="c38e74dc-bc49-482d-a200-9a163da4dac5-link">10</a></sup> simplifies device discovery and connection, and <strong>Link to Windows</strong><sup data-fn="c7b2f803-dca7-445e-ae8a-15323aa9cd0c" class="fn"><a href="#c7b2f803-dca7-445e-ae8a-15323aa9cd0c" id="c7b2f803-dca7-445e-ae8a-15323aa9cd0c-link">11</a></sup> extends supported Galaxy smartphone experiences to the PC.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="639" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28161959/Samsung-Mobile-The-new-Galaxy-Book6-Galaxy-Book6-LIneup-Galaxy-AI_main7.jpg" alt="Quick Share and Storage Share make it easy to move content across Galaxy devices, while Multi Control and Second Screen help users work seamlessly across screens." class="wp-image-176864" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Quick Share and Storage Share make it easy to move content across Galaxy devices, while Multi Control and Second Screen help users work seamlessly across screens.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Together, Galaxy AI and connected Galaxy experiences reduce the steps between devices, helping users stay focused as information, content, and work move more seamlessly across the Galaxy ecosystem.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Confidence That Lasts Beyond the Purchase</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Book6 is backed by Samsung’s commitment to long-term reliability, with quality, durability, and support designed to give users confidence over time. Every Galaxy Book6 undergoes rigorous durability and quality testing to meet Samsung’s highest standards. For added peace of mind, Samsung Care+<sup data-fn="1419819f-f578-4742-82e2-528a8d81a7b8" class="fn"><a href="#1419819f-f578-4742-82e2-528a8d81a7b8" id="1419819f-f578-4742-82e2-528a8d81a7b8-link">12</a></sup> offers comprehensive coverage for accidental damage, repairs, and replacements, helping users feel more confident throughout the life of their PC.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Book6 is available in select markets, with additional markets to follow. Starting at $799, it offers 8GB or 16GB of memory and 256GB, 512GB, or 1TB SSD storage options. Availability, specifications, colors, features, and supported experiences may vary by region.</p>



<p></p>

				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-hosts-playgalaxy-cup-world-final-at-gamescom-2026</link>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that it hosted the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final from Aug. 27-28, and that the final was held at the company’s booth as part of its larger exhibition at Gamescom 2026, held at Koelnmesse in Cologne, Germany, from Aug. 26-30. The #PlayGalaxy Cup is a global mobile gaming tournament designed to showcase […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it hosted the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final from Aug. 27-28, and that the final was held at the company’s booth as part of its larger exhibition at Gamescom 2026, held at Koelnmesse in Cologne, Germany, from Aug. 26-30.</p>



<p>The #PlayGalaxy Cup is a global mobile gaming tournament designed to showcase the powerful gaming performance of Galaxy smartphones. This year marked the first time the competition was held as a full-season league, with regional qualifiers leading up to the World Final.</p>



<p> “This year was the first time the #PlayGalaxy Cup was held as a full season tournament, and we’ve witnessed thrilling competition from start to finish,” said David Moon, Head of Influencer Marketing, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Samsung is thrilled to have brought gamers from all over the world together to experience the powerful performance of Galaxy S26 Ultra.”</p>



<p>The regional qualifiers were held across five regions — North America, Southwest Asia, Southeast Asia, Latin America, and Europe — with 32 teams from 17 countries competing for a place in the final stage. The top two teams from each region advanced, bringing a total of 10 teams from nine countries to the World Final at Gamescom.</p>



<p>After proving their skills through the regional qualifiers, the finalists then competed over two days for the championship.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/31100824/Samsung-Mobile-PlayGalaxy-Cup-2026-Gamescom-2026-World-Final-Galaxy-S26-Ultra_main1.jpg" alt="Players compete at the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final during Gamescom 2026." class="wp-image-176901" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Players compete at the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final during Gamescom 2026.</figcaption></figure>



<p>All players at the World Final competed on Galaxy S26 Ultra, whose powerful processing performance and optimized thermal management enabled high-intensity matches through stable, immersive gameplay.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/31100837/Samsung-Mobile-PlayGalaxy-Cup-2026-Gamescom-2026-World-Final-Galaxy-S26-Ultra_main2.jpg" alt="Streamer Ludwig hosts the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final at Samsung’s Gamescom 2026 booth." class="wp-image-176902" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Streamer Ludwig hosts the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final at Samsung’s Gamescom 2026 booth.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The final was won by Malaysian team and was livestreamed worldwide on YouTube through streamer Ludwig’s channel, drawing a total of 12.1M views.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="561" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/31100903/Samsung-Mobile-PlayGalaxy-Cup-2026-Gamescom-2026-World-Final-Galaxy-S26-Ultra_main3.jpg" alt="The Malaysian team celebrates its victory at the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final. (From left) San Gaming, VRT1CAA, ADIKBOY, and ACAD" class="wp-image-176903" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The Malaysian team celebrates its victory at the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final. (From left) San Gaming, VRT1CAA, ADIKBOY, and ACAD</figcaption></figure>



<p>In addition to the #PlayGalaxy Cup World Final, Samsung operated a dedicated Galaxy experience zone at the venue, where visitors could get hands-on experience with Galaxy S26 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra.</p>



<p>Through partnerships with HoYoverse, TiMi Studio, and Gameloft, visitors to Samsung’s booth were also able to experience a variety of popular game titles across Galaxy devices.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Galaxy S26 FE: Delivering the Latest Flagship Experience, Focused on What Matters Most]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-galaxy-s26-fe-delivering-the-latest-flagship-experience-focused-on-what-matters-most</link>
				<pubDate>Thu, 27 Aug 2026 21:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/27192634/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-S26-FE_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy AI]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy S26 FE]]></category>

                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4xRsq2s</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced Galaxy S26 FE, the newest addition to the Galaxy S26 family and the first in the lineup to launch with One UI 9 — bringing the latest premium Galaxy experiences to more users from day one. With enhanced camera capabilities and more context-aware Galaxy AI, it elevates how users capture, create, […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced Galaxy S26 FE, the newest addition to the Galaxy S26 family and the first in the lineup to launch with One UI 9 — bringing the latest premium Galaxy experiences to more users from day one. With enhanced camera capabilities and more context-aware Galaxy AI, it elevates how users capture, create, and connect every day. Built around what users value most in a thin and light design, Galaxy S26 FE carries the iconic Galaxy S experience forward.</p>



<p>“Galaxy S26 FE is purposefully designed to deliver the signature strengths of the Galaxy S series, ensuring that every feature makes a meaningful difference in users’ daily lives,” said Jay Kim, Executive Vice President and Head of Customer Experience Office, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Packed with beloved Galaxy camera and AI experiences, backed by the latest One UI 9, Galaxy S26 FE comes to life as an ideal choice for those who know exactly what matters most to them.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/27114304/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-S26-FE_Main1.gif" alt="Galaxy S26 FE " class="wp-image-176821" /></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Sharper Capture, Effortless Editing</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy S26 FE delivers a more capable camera experience<sup data-fn="8e83c206-ee9b-45e2-b617-0bf3f10a8a38" class="fn"><a href="#8e83c206-ee9b-45e2-b617-0bf3f10a8a38" id="8e83c206-ee9b-45e2-b617-0bf3f10a8a38-link">1</a></sup> from capture to edit, with an enhanced triple rear camera and advanced image processing that help users capture and refine the moments that matter — whether shooting still scenes or fast-moving action.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/27114501/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-S26-FE_Main2.gif" alt="The feature of My FanCam" class="wp-image-176822" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>My FanCam,</strong><sup data-fn="6c9eb2ba-f7ca-476a-a091-cc7b66813a5a" class="fn"><a href="#6c9eb2ba-f7ca-476a-a091-cc7b66813a5a" id="6c9eb2ba-f7ca-476a-a091-cc7b66813a5a-link">2</a></sup> first introduced on the latest Galaxy foldables, is now available on Galaxy S26 FE. It automatically tracks a selected person and keeps them centered, intelligently reframing video as the scene moves, so users can turn dynamic moments into social-ready content with less manual editing.</li>



<li>For video, <strong>Super Steady</strong><sup data-fn="acbab68e-09e2-4356-ac84-78c5589ff2bf" class="fn"><a href="#acbab68e-09e2-4356-ac84-78c5589ff2bf" id="acbab68e-09e2-4356-ac84-78c5589ff2bf-link">3</a></sup> with Horizontal Lock — a favorite among Galaxy S26 users — helps capture smoother, more level footage while filming fast-moving activities, travel moments, and everyday scenes on the go.</li>



<li>Galaxy S26 FE supports <strong>3x optical zoom</strong>, giving users more confidence when shooting from a distance. For selfies, an enhanced <strong>12 MP front camera</strong><sup data-fn="039e66d8-82ee-4e23-b1d4-0ee9d0122c45" class="fn"><a href="#039e66d8-82ee-4e23-b1d4-0ee9d0122c45" id="039e66d8-82ee-4e23-b1d4-0ee9d0122c45-link">4</a></sup> captures more in every frame, making group shots easier to take.</li>



<li><strong>Nightography</strong>, a signature of the Galaxy S series, helps users capture bright and clear shots in low light, with detail preserved even in the most challenging scenes.<sup data-fn="2f6d9c50-ee82-40e8-b476-e3d613574ad7" class="fn"><a href="#2f6d9c50-ee82-40e8-b476-e3d613574ad7" id="2f6d9c50-ee82-40e8-b476-e3d613574ad7-link">5</a></sup></li>



<li>Even after the moment has passed, <strong>Photo Assist</strong><sup data-fn="c3604388-2d2b-4149-ab0d-4180190956ff" class="fn"><a href="#c3604388-2d2b-4149-ab0d-4180190956ff" id="c3604388-2d2b-4149-ab0d-4180190956ff-link">6</a></sup> makes it easier to refine images using natural language prompts — turning day into night, adding new elements or restoring missing parts. Plus, edits can be reviewed step by step and revised or undone along the way.</li>



<li>With <strong>Gemini Omni</strong>,<sup data-fn="830918db-e800-4409-a099-736605e0c7c8" class="fn"><a href="#830918db-e800-4409-a099-736605e0c7c8" id="830918db-e800-4409-a099-736605e0c7c8-link">7</a></sup> users can easily create and edit videos clips based on photo and videos from their Gallery, turning them into polished, ready-to-share videos.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/27114535/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-S26-FE_Main3.gif" alt="The feature of Photo Assist" class="wp-image-176823" /></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI Experiences, Always One Step Ahead</strong></h2>



<p>Powered by the latest One UI 9, Galaxy S26 FE brings a range of intuitive, context-aware Galaxy AI<sup data-fn="2024ccef-d13c-46ff-a539-0103a6a87801" class="fn"><a href="#2024ccef-d13c-46ff-a539-0103a6a87801" id="2024ccef-d13c-46ff-a539-0103a6a87801-link">8</a></sup> experiences that make daily life easier by adapting to users’ routines to keep them informed and organized. It also handles everyday tasks faster and more seamlessly on user’s behalf.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Updated <strong>Now Brief,</strong><sup data-fn="f49e5d09-8a0d-43c9-865d-d52b2bf37a9f" class="fn"><a href="#f49e5d09-8a0d-43c9-865d-d52b2bf37a9f" id="f49e5d09-8a0d-43c9-865d-d52b2bf37a9f-link">9</a></sup> first introduced on the Galaxy S25 series, is even more personalized through customizable briefing cards like weather and wellness insights. Users can now create their own cards with a simple prompt, making Now Brief more relevant throughout the day.</li>



<li><strong>Now Nudge</strong><sup data-fn="91bd908c-f64e-47bc-aec0-06ca9b84c52e" class="fn"><a href="#91bd908c-f64e-47bc-aec0-06ca9b84c52e" id="91bd908c-f64e-47bc-aec0-06ca9b84c52e-link">10</a></sup> is expanded to select third-party apps and notifications, surfacing timely, relevant suggestions across more of the day — helping users turn insights into action and manage everyday routines with less friction.</li>



<li><strong>Circle to Search with Google</strong><sup data-fn="a4f7de40-3f32-4919-8ea2-5187aa5f251c" class="fn"><a href="#a4f7de40-3f32-4919-8ea2-5187aa5f251c" id="a4f7de40-3f32-4919-8ea2-5187aa5f251c-link">11</a></sup><strong> </strong>now allows users to search for multiple items in an image at once, making it easier to identify products, places, objects and more in a single step.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Built to Keep Up Throughout the Day</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy S26 FE brings meaningful upgrades to the everyday essentials users rely on most — including a long-lasting battery, and a bright display built for work, entertainment, and creativity from morning to night.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>A <strong>long-lasting 4,900 mAh battery</strong><sup data-fn="6bd1c2b2-af1a-4e8f-8688-bde1bcededac" class="fn"><a href="#6bd1c2b2-af1a-4e8f-8688-bde1bcededac" id="6bd1c2b2-af1a-4e8f-8688-bde1bcededac-link">12</a></sup> keeps users going while maintaining the slim and refined design of the Galaxy S26 lineup. Users can reach up to 69% charge in around 30 minutes with the 45 W adapter,<sup data-fn="36067999-a3a3-48ad-b1a6-e2931e54aa34" class="fn"><a href="#36067999-a3a3-48ad-b1a6-e2931e54aa34" id="36067999-a3a3-48ad-b1a6-e2931e54aa34-link">13</a></sup> powering up quickly to stay connected for longer.</li>



<li>A <strong>6.7-inch</strong><sup data-fn="682c8295-348a-41a6-8129-0f66a184c7b4" class="fn"><a href="#682c8295-348a-41a6-8129-0f66a184c7b4" id="682c8295-348a-41a6-8129-0f66a184c7b4-link">14</a></sup> <strong>AMOLED</strong> <strong>display</strong> with up to <strong>120 Hz refresh rate</strong> provides smooth, immersive visuals with brightness, resolution, and clarity for streaming, gaming, browsing, and creative work.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="725" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/27114601/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-S26-FE_Main4.jpg" alt="An image of a 6.7-inch AMOLED display for immersive viewing" class="wp-image-176824" /></figure>



<p>Users choosing Galaxy S26 FE as their next device will feel the power of the Galaxy experience from day one. An improved Smart Switch helps users get set up more easily, making the move to Galaxy feel like a warm welcome. Meanwhile, long-term software updates and Samsung Care+ provide added confidence over time.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Upgraded <strong>Smart Switch</strong><sup data-fn="452cffaf-e4b0-4ff6-838f-06545e27f59d" class="fn"><a href="#452cffaf-e4b0-4ff6-838f-06545e27f59d" id="452cffaf-e4b0-4ff6-838f-06545e27f59d-link">15</a></sup> makes moving to Galaxy easier than ever. Whether switching from an Android or iOS device, users can scan a QR code to wirelessly transfer data — from passwords and passkeys to call history, accessibility settings, and eSIM data — with no extra apps and less setup time.</li>



<li>Galaxy S26 FE is built to last, with up to <strong>seven generations of OS upgrades and seven years of security updates,</strong><sup data-fn="7d104a5f-72b8-4db0-bb3a-c1e530d303fe" class="fn"><a href="#7d104a5f-72b8-4db0-bb3a-c1e530d303fe" id="7d104a5f-72b8-4db0-bb3a-c1e530d303fe-link">16</a></sup> allowing users to enjoy the latest features with confidence for years to come.</li>



<li>For added peace of mind, <strong>Samsung Care+</strong><sup data-fn="7d4dd48c-422d-41bd-bd42-c7eb5dddd830" class="fn"><a href="#7d4dd48c-422d-41bd-bd42-c7eb5dddd830" id="7d4dd48c-422d-41bd-bd42-c7eb5dddd830-link">17</a></sup> offers comprehensive coverage optimized to users’ needs, including tailored benefits that protect the value of the device. With One UI 9, the new Warranty and care settings hub also makes it easy to enroll in, access, and manage Samsung Care+, along with other device care support.</li>



<li>A 6-month free trial of <strong>Google AI Pro</strong><sup data-fn="4ffcdeef-1760-4bf5-ae0b-ed6a5eb6352a" class="fn"><a href="#4ffcdeef-1760-4bf5-ae0b-ed6a5eb6352a" id="4ffcdeef-1760-4bf5-ae0b-ed6a5eb6352a-link">18</a></sup> will bring expanded AI capabilities and 5 TB of cloud storage valued at USD 19.99 per month.</li>
</ul>



<p>Together, these enhancements deliver the Galaxy experiences users rely on every day in one thoughtfully crafted device.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy S26 FE will be available starting <strong>Sept. 4</strong> in select markets in three colors: Blueberry, Graphite, and Pistachio.<sup data-fn="064d1b22-41f8-4c35-ba10-7562cfe2d123" class="fn"><a href="#064d1b22-41f8-4c35-ba10-7562cfe2d123" id="064d1b22-41f8-4c35-ba10-7562cfe2d123-link">19</a></sup></p>
