									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that it will establish a FläktGroup HVAC production line in Korea to target the rapidly growing HVAC market, including the production of advanced cooling solutions for AI data centers. The company plans to invest approximately KRW 240 billion (USD 158 million) to build a 21,500m2 HVAC production line at its operations […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="684" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18141700/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics(Gwangju)" class="wp-image-176546" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it will establish a FläktGroup HVAC production line in Korea to target the rapidly growing HVAC market, including the production of advanced cooling solutions for AI data centers.</p>



<p>The company plans to invest approximately KRW 240 billion (USD 158 million) to build a 21,500m<sup>2</sup> HVAC production line at its operations site in Gwangju, Korea. The new production line is scheduled to begin operations upon completion by 2028. It will manufacture HVAC products from FläktGroup, which was acquired by Samsung Electronics <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-electronics-acquires-leading-global-hvac-solutions-provider-flaktgroup" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">last year.</a></p>



<p>“Samsung’s acquisition of FläktGroup last year puts us in a unique position to expand the HVAC business and transform it into a new growth engine, to become a global top-tier company by 2030,” said Cheolgi Kim, Executive Vice President and Head of Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “This investment in the new production line in Korea will serve as a solid foundation for achieving this goal.”</p>



<p>FläktGroup recently invested in expanding its manufacturing footprint – which will operate soon – through Project GAGAN, a new production facility in Pune, Maharashtra, India. With a global network of 14 manufacturing facilities, the company actively supplies products across key markets including the U.S., Europe and India.</p>



<p>Together with FläktGroup, Samsung will leverage the establishment of the Gwangju production line, its 15th manufacturing facility worldwide, as a strategic springboard for a full-scale expansion into the Korean market.</p>



<p>Samsung plans to supply HVAC facilities and infrastructure across various industrial sectors in Korea, with a particular emphasis on the surge in demand for AI data centers. To achieve this, the company will produce advanced HVAC products, including the production of advanced cooling solutions for AI data centers.</p>



<p>David Dorney, CEO of FläktGroup, said, “By combining Samsung’s AI expertise with FläktGroup’s HVAC product capabilities, we will be positioned to become an HVAC partner of choice in Korea and across Asia.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18142459/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main2.jpg" alt="Planned site for FläktGroup’s HVAC Production Line" class="wp-image-176547" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="788" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18142511/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main3.jpg" alt="HVAC (CDU 1)" class="wp-image-176548" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="480" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18142523/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main4.jpg" alt="HVAC (CDU 2)" class="wp-image-176549" /></figure>
]]></content:encoded>

																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>When summer temperatures soar, there’s nothing quite like a refreshing drink or chilled treat to cool down. From iced coffee and fizzy sodas to chilled smoothies and sorbets, ice is an essential ingredient in summer favorites.</p>



<p>But different foods and drinks call for different types of ice. Small, crushed ice is ideal for quickly chilling a beverage, while larger, slower-melting ice helps keep it cold for longer. For larger ice such as sphere ice, clarity is another key characteristic.</p>



<p>Across its global Bespoke AI Refrigerator lineup, Samsung Electronics offers six types of ice designed with different drinking cultures and preferences in mind. Available ice types vary by region and model. Select models also feature technology that precisely controls the freezing rate to create clear, solid sphere ice. Check out the infographics below to learn more about Samsung’s carefully engineered ice-making technology, thoughtfully engineered down to the shape and transparency of the ice.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="1000" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14174214/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Bespoke-AI-Refrigerators-Ice-Making-Technology-Infographic_main1.gif" alt="Across its global Bespoke AI Refrigerator lineup, Samsung Electronics offers six types of ice." class="wp-image-176515" /></figure>

<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="1000" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14174234/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Bespoke-AI-Refrigerators-Ice-Making-Technology-Infographic_main3.jpg" alt="Ice size" class="wp-image-176517" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="1000" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14174245/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Bespoke-AI-Refrigerators-Ice-Making-Technology-Infographic_main4.jpg" alt="Cubed, Crushed, Sphere, and Ice Bites" class="wp-image-176518" /></figure>

				<title><![CDATA[‘Warranty and care’ Menu Simplifies Device Care for New Foldables]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/warranty-and-care-menu-simplifies-device-care-for-new-foldables</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 18 Aug 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14154108/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Flip8]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[One UI 9]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Care+]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Warranty and care]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4hyZ3NG</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung users may have noticed something new on their Galaxy Z foldables — the Warranty and care menu. Managing warranties and coverage for a Galaxy mobile phone, Galaxy Watch, Galaxy Buds, and Galaxy Tab can be a hassle. That’s where Warranty and care comes in. Introduced with One UI 9, it brings essential device management […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung users may have noticed something new on their <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-galaxy-z-fold8-ultra-fold8-and-flip8foldables-perfected-for-every-way-of-living" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Galaxy Z foldables</a> — the <strong>Warranty and care</strong><sup data-fn="5a7d6cfe-cd00-43cf-a6ab-7e75fd5e6cb4" class="fn"><a href="#5a7d6cfe-cd00-43cf-a6ab-7e75fd5e6cb4" id="5a7d6cfe-cd00-43cf-a6ab-7e75fd5e6cb4-link">1</a></sup> menu.</p>



<p>Managing warranties and coverage for a Galaxy mobile phone, Galaxy Watch, Galaxy Buds, and Galaxy Tab can be a hassle. That’s where Warranty and care comes in. Introduced with <strong>One UI 9</strong>, it brings essential device management features together in one place, letting users check warranty status, run diagnostics, book repairs, and get support in just a few taps — all without leaving Settings.</p>



<p>Here’s a closer look at what Warranty and care, available with One UI 9 on the Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8, offers.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Check Warranty Status Across Galaxy Devices</strong></h2>



<p>Keeping track of warranty periods and expiration dates across Galaxy devices isn’t always easy. Warranty and care consolidates this information in a single hub.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171312/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main1.jpg" alt="Warranty and care consolidates this information in a single hub." class="wp-image-176504" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171342/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main2.jpg" alt="Access Warranty and care instantly from Settings." class="wp-image-176505" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Access Warranty and care instantly from Settings.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Available in Settings, Warranty and care displays information<sup data-fn="1c040dc1-8d25-426e-9599-0b77894733a1" class="fn"><a href="#1c040dc1-8d25-426e-9599-0b77894733a1" id="1c040dc1-8d25-426e-9599-0b77894733a1-link">2</a></sup> about the device in use at the top of the screen — including remaining warranty period and eligibility for <a href="https://www.samsung.com/sec/samsung-care-plus/im/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>Samsung Care+</strong></a><strong> </strong>enrollment.<sup data-fn="72a9207a-0daa-4156-84e6-71cb0ac0614a" class="fn"><a href="#72a9207a-0daa-4156-84e6-71cb0ac0614a" id="72a9207a-0daa-4156-84e6-71cb0ac0614a-link">3</a></sup> Below, users can find the same details for other Galaxy devices linked to their Samsung account, making it easy to check warranty and coverage status across their Galaxy ecosystem.</p>



<p>For added protection beyond the standard warranty, users can easily explore and enroll in a Samsung Care+ plan that matches their needs.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Resolve Device Issues With Diagnostics and Bixby</strong></h2>



<p>When something doesn’t feel quite right, quickly finding out what’s wrong can make all the difference. The built-in <strong>Diagnostics</strong> feature checks the device — including its battery, charging, sensors, touch response, and connectivity — to help pinpoint the issue and guide users to the next step, whether that’s a suggested solution or a repair request.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171559/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main3.jpg" alt="Bixby offers customized responses to your inquiries." class="wp-image-176506" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Bixby offers customized responses to your inquiries.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Users can also access <strong>Bixby</strong><sup data-fn="29ae218e-01b6-439f-a48b-2dd3114788a2" class="fn"><a href="#29ae218e-01b6-439f-a48b-2dd3114788a2" id="29ae218e-01b6-439f-a48b-2dd3114788a2-link">4</a></sup> from Warranty and care to ask about an issue and get help on the spot. For example, if a user asks, “My battery is draining too fast. What should I do?” Bixby can walk them through possible solutions step by step. It’s a convenient way to get answers to everyday questions without switching screens.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Book Repairs and Check Estimated Costs in One Place</strong></h2>



<p>When professional help is needed, users can tap the “Request a repair” to book a repair at a <strong>Samsung-authorized service center</strong> without having to make phone calls. All repairs are carried out by Samsung-certified technicians using genuine parts. If users are unable to visit a service center, they can start remote support from the same screen and get help directly on their device.</p>



<p>Warranty and care also makes device care service information easy to check. After selecting an issue, users can view estimated repair costs<sup data-fn="768ae12d-0d07-4065-afd6-afd8b9dcf7d6" class="fn"><a href="#768ae12d-0d07-4065-afd6-afd8b9dcf7d6" id="768ae12d-0d07-4065-afd6-afd8b9dcf7d6-link">5</a></sup> for major parts alongside the cost of the same repair with Samsung Care+. This comparison helps users weigh their options and decide whether additional protection is worthwhile.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171637/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main4.jpg" alt="Warranty and care also makes device care service information easy to check." class="wp-image-176507" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171651/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main5.jpg" alt="Repair estimates on main parts can also be verified." class="wp-image-176508" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Repair estimates on main parts can also be verified.</figcaption></figure>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Protect Device Value for the Long Term</strong></h2>



<p>By bringing warranty information, diagnostics, support, and repair services together, Warranty and care gives Galaxy users a simpler way to manage their devices for greater peace of mind. Checking the feature during setup makes it easier to know where to turn if an issue comes up later — from running diagnostics and asking Bixby or a chatbot for help to requesting a repair. With these essential services close at hand, users can keep their devices in good condition for longer and help preserve their value.</p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Partners With Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna To Bring Austrian Masterpieces to Samsung Art Store]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-partners-with-kunsthistorisches-museum-in-vienna-to-bring-austrian-masterpieces-to-samsung-art-store</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 18 Aug 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173531/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[TVs & Displays]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Art Store]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung TV]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/3Uw53Nq</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics and the Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna today announced a new partnership to bring 43 carefully selected masterpieces from the museum’s collection to Samsung Art Store. Starting today, art enthusiasts worldwide will be able to access these iconic works in stunning 4K resolution exclusively on compatible Samsung TVs. “The Kunsthistorisches Museum is not merely […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13172859/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main1.jpg" alt="(From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto the Elder on OLED TV, “Summer” (1563) by Giuseppe Arcimboldo on The Frame and “The Tower of Babel” (1563) by Pieter Bruegel the Elder on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna. " class="wp-image-176470" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto the Elder on OLED TV, “Summer” (1563) by Giuseppe Arcimboldo on The Frame and “The Tower of Babel” (1563) by Pieter Bruegel the Elder on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna. Photo: Seniad Kasupovic</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics and the Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna today announced a new partnership to bring 43 carefully selected masterpieces from the museum’s collection to Samsung Art Store.<sup data-fn="240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3" class="fn"><a href="#240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3" id="240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3-link">1</a></sup> Starting today, art enthusiasts worldwide will be able to access these iconic works in stunning 4K resolution exclusively on compatible Samsung TVs.<sup data-fn="ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907" class="fn"><a href="#ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907" id="ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907-link">2</a></sup></p>



<p>“The Kunsthistorisches Museum is not merely an institution — it’s a symbol of Austrian cultural identity and is our neighbor, our partner and our home,” said Dario Casari, President of Samsung Austria and Samsung Switzerland. “This partnership represents something truly special for Samsung Austria — by combining these world-class cultural treasures with Samsung’s cutting-edge display technology, we’re creating a unique bridge between history and innovation, bringing Vienna’s artistic legacy to audiences around the globe.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173113/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main2.jpg" alt="(From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace”, known as “Canaletto View” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto on OLED TV and “The Art of Painting” (1665-68) by Johannes Vermeer on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum. " class="wp-image-176471" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace”, known as “Canaletto View” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto on OLED TV and “The Art of Painting” (1665-68) by Johannes Vermeer on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum. Photo: Seniad Kasupovic</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Masterpieces from Bruegel to Vermeer Now Available Digitally</strong></h2>



<p>The Samsung Art Store collection features some of the most significant works from the Kunsthistorisches Museum’s painting gallery, including:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Several paintings by Pieter Bruegel the Elder, including the famous “The Tower of Babel”</li>



<li>Johannes Vermeer’s “The Art of Painting”</li>



<li>Gustav Klimt’s “Nuda Veritas”</li>



<li>Canaletto and Bellotto’s iconic views of Venice and Vienna</li>



<li>Giuseppe Arcimboldo’s “Summer”</li>



<li>Works by Rubens, Velázquez, Caravaggio, Titian, Rembrandt, and Raphael</li>
</ul>



<p>Thanks to high-resolution digital rendering, viewers can appreciate the finest details of these extraordinary works. Bruegel’s “The Tower of Babel” reveals countless architectural and technical details, while Vermeer’s “The Art of Painting” impressively showcases the artist’s distinctive use of light and precision.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="583" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173210/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main3.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-176472" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ “The Tower of Babel” (1563) by Pieter Bruegel the Elder on The Frame Pro.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Bringing World-Class Cultural Heritage Directly to the Home</strong></h2>



<p>With the Kunsthistorisches Museum joining the Samsung Art Store platform, Samsung continues to expand its premium portfolio of international cultural institutions. Existing collaborations with renowned museums such as the Louvre in Paris, the Prado Museum in Madrid, and the MUNCH Museum in Oslo already offer users access to unique art collections directly on their Samsung TVs.</p>



<p>Samsung Art Store offers a curated selection of thousands of international artworks displayed in native resolution with precise color calibration. Users can personalize their viewing experience by selecting artworks according to style, period or mood, transforming their living spaces into their own digital gallery.</p>



<p>“We are delighted to join Samsung Art Store and share our unparalleled collection with a global audience,” said Dr. Jonathan Fine, General Director of KHM-Museumsverband. “By making these masterpieces accessible to everyone, regardless of location, we can engage a wider public. This digital experience will fascinate viewers and inspire them to encounter the original artworks in person at our museum in Vienna, bridging the gap between digital accessibility and the irreplaceable experience of standing before the originals.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173301/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main4.jpg" alt="Back row, from left: Manuel Laporta Osante, Head of TV/AV - Samsung Austria, Verena Eisner, Deputy CFO - The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Antonio Marques, Strategy Product Manager - Samsung Austria. Front row, from left: Paul Frey, General Manager - The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Dario Casari, President - Samsung Austria and Samsung Switzerland." class="wp-image-176474" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Back row, from left: Manuel Laporta Osante, Head of TV/AV – Samsung Austria, Verena Eisner, Deputy CFO – The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Antonio Marques, Strategy Product Manager – Samsung Austria. Front row, from left: Paul Frey, General Manager – The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Dario Casari, President – Samsung Austria and Samsung Switzerland.</figcaption></figure>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong><u>About the Kunsthistorisches Museum</u></strong><br>The Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna is one of the world’s most important universal museums. Since its opening in 1891, it has housed the Habsburg collections and showcases artworks ranging from ancient Egyptian and classical antiquities to masterpieces of European painting. The museum houses the world’s largest collection of paintings by Pieter Bruegel the Elder. The museum building itself is an architectural masterpiece and one of the most impressive landmarks along Vienna’s historic Ringstrasse.</h4>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13170606/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Gamescom-2026-HDR10-GAMING-Call-of-Duty-Modern-Warfare-4_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics announced an exclusive partnership with Activision as the official TV, monitor, soundbar and Wi-Fi speaker of Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4." class="wp-image-176464" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced an exclusive partnership with Activision as the official TV, monitor, soundbar, and Wi-Fi speaker of Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4, the latest title in one of the world’s largest first-person shooter (FPS) franchises. The collaboration brings Call of Duty together with Samsung’s premium display and connected gaming technologies to give players more ways to experience the title before its official launch.</p>



<p>Call of Duty is a critically acclaimed and award-winning franchise that has become one of gaming’s most recognized intellectual properties. The collaboration marks another step in Samsung’s efforts to expand next-generation gaming experiences across TVs, monitors, soundbars, Wi-Fi speakers, and connected gaming services.</p>



<p>“We are pleased to be the official TV, monitor, soundbar and Wi-Fi speaker partner for Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4, bringing players a more immersive experience through Samsung’s premium display and audio technologies,” said Hun Lee, Executive Vice President of the Visual Display (VD) Business at Samsung Electronics. “We will continue working with leading developers to strengthen gaming experiences across our display portfolio.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>HDR10+ GAMING Brings Greater Detail to Gameplay</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung and Call of Duty will integrate <strong>HDR10+ GAMING</strong> into Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4. The technology helps deliver more accurate color and contrast, supporting the immersive visual experience that reflects the creative intent of the developers.</p>



<p>“Samsung’s display and gaming technologies provide an exciting way to experience Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4,” said Cody Neal, Head of Partnerships at Activision. “Partnering with Samsung allows us to showcase the game with clarity, scale, and responsiveness that complement the intensity at the heart of Call of Duty.”</p>



<p>Modern Warfare 4 will officially support a <strong>32:9 super ultrawide aspect ratio</strong>, allowing Samsung Odyssey G9 users to experience a wider format giving players a broader sense of space in-game.</p>



<p>Samsung will also give consumers around the world more ways to experience Modern Warfare 4 across Samsung TVs, Odyssey monitors, and gaming services.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Samsung Gaming Hub Streaming Call of Duty: NEXT</strong></h2>



<p>As part of the collaboration, <strong>Samsung Gaming Hub</strong> users will be able to watch Call of Duty: NEXT live through Samsung Gaming Hub on Aug. 21 at 9 am PST. Ahead of the event, a tune-in banner promoting Call of Duty: NEXT will be featured in the Samsung TV Gaming Hub masthead.</p>



<p>Samsung will also distribute Beta Early Access codes for Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 through the Samsung Promotion app. The codes will give selected players an opportunity to experience the Modern Warfare 4 Beta from Aug. 21-25, before the Open Beta begins, starting Aug. 28 through Sep. 1. Users can check for Beta Early Access code availability and event details on the Samsung TV Gaming Hub.</p>



<p>Samsung Gaming Hub allows users to access major cloud gaming services, including Xbox Cloud Gaming, as well as other game-related content, in one place. With it, users can access supported services and content without switching devices.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Gamescom 2026: Samsung Odyssey To Showcase Speed and Detail of Modern Warfare 4</strong></h2>



<p>Additionally, Samsung will bring Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 to its booth for an early hands-on experience during Gamescom 2026, taking place Aug. 26-30 in Cologne, Germany. As one of the world’s leading events for computer and video games, Gamescom brings together players, creators, publishers, and technology brands from across the industry.</p>



<p>At the event, Samsung will provide Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 Vault Edition Battle.net game codes to select players who visit its booth. Visitors will also be able to play the highly anticipated title ahead of its official October 23 release on premium Samsung Odyssey gaming monitors. Built for modern gaming, Odyssey monitors give players the visual clarity and smooth performance needed for competitive play and cinematic moments.</p>



<p>For more information, visit <a href="http://www.samsung.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">www.samsung.com</a>.</p>

					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[From Biosignals to Health Insights: Samsung Research’s Work on Health Foundation Models]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/from-biosignals-to-health-insights-samsung-researchs-work-on-health-foundation-models</link>
				<pubDate>Fri, 14 Aug 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13165926/Samsung-Corporate-Biosignals-Health-Insights-Samsung-Research-Health-Foundation-Models_Thumb1400-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Technology]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Unpacked July 2026]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Watch]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Watch9]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Health]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4g2E053</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[AI is being widely used to analyze biosignals measured by wearable devices such as smartwatches. By identifying meaningful patterns in health data — including sleep, heart rate, and physical activity — AI is helping users better understand and manage their health. The Health Forum held at Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 also shared Samsung’s Connected Care […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>AI is being widely used to analyze biosignals measured by wearable devices such as smartwatches. By identifying meaningful patterns in health data — including sleep, heart rate, and physical activity — AI is helping users better understand and manage their health.</p>



<p><a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/galaxy-unpacked-july-2026-samsung-and-partners-envision-a-connected-care-future-from-first-signal-to-lasting-change" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">The Health Forum held at Galaxy Unpacked July 2026</a> also shared Samsung’s Connected Care vision for the next chapter of digital health — a future where care moves from reactive treatment toward preventive, personalized, and connected experiences, supported by trusted health innovation and partnerships across the healthcare ecosystem. One technology that is helping power these new consumer experiences is the health foundation model.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="600" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13151035/Samsung-Corporate-Biosignals-Health-Insights-Samsung-Research-Health-Foundation-Models_Main1.jpg" alt="Foundation Model " class="wp-image-176439" /></figure>



<p>Researchers from the Digital Health Team at Samsung Research America (SRA) are developing new AI technologies to continuously understand a person’s health state from biosignals, generate health insights, and offer appropriate health guidance.</p>



<p>Samsung researchers recently introduced two foundation models based on wearable data: <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00973" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">xMAE (Physiology-Aware Masked Cross-Modal Reconstruction for Biosignal Representation Learning)</a>, which learns the temporal relationship between different biosignals, and <a href="https://iclr.cc/virtual/2026/poster/10007966" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">HiMAE (Hierarchical Masked Autoencoder)</a>, which understands health patterns across different time scales in wearable time-series data. Both studies demonstrate advancement of health AI models that can better understand the physiological relationships and temporal structure of biosignals.</p>



<p>Samsung’s work on xMAE and HiMAE models was accepted to the International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML), and the International Conference on Learning Representations (ICLR), respectively, highlighting the significance of this research.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Health Foundation Models That Read the Body’s Signals: Why They Matter for Health </strong></h2>



<p>A health foundation model is an AI model that uses self-supervised learning to learn meaningful features from unlabeled biosignal data. After being pretrained on large-scale health data, the models can be applied to perform a wide range of downstream health tasks, including biosignal analysis, developing new or improved biomarkers, and health issue prediction.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>xMAE AI model: Continuous Cardiac Insight from Wearables</strong></h2>



<p>An electrocardiogram (ECG) on wearables directly measures the electrical activity of the heart and is useful for measuring heart rate and heart rate variability, identifying abnormal heart rhythms and risks for conditions such as atrial fibrillation. ECG is highly accurate, but typically requires users to pause and take an active measurement. On the other hand, photoplethysmography (PPG) can indirectly measure cardiac function by detecting changes in blood flow and can be passively and continuously measured through sensors in wearable devices such as smartwatches.</p>



<p>The two signals originate from the same cardiac activity, but they appear with a certain time difference, much like thunder is heard after lightning is seen. xMAE is a biosignal pretraining framework designed to learn the temporal relationship between the two signals by reconstructing masked portions of the ECG signal using the more easily and continuously measured PPG signal. As a result, cardiovascular health-related features can be analyzed more precisely using PPG without requiring separate manual ECG measurements.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="780" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13152042/Samsung-Corporate-Biosignals-Health-Insights-Samsung-Research-Health-Foundation-Models_Main2.jpg" alt="Image of xMAE AI Model " class="wp-image-176440" /></figure>



<p>The researchers pretrained xMAE using approximately 9,400 hours of ECG and PPG data. The model outperformed unimodal biosignal models and existing multimodal learning methods in 15 of 19 evaluation tasks, including cardiovascular disease prediction, abnormal test-result detection, and sleep-stage classification. The study also confirmed the potential to use the learned features across different sensor devices, body locations and data-gathering environments.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>HiMAE AI model: Reading the Body at Every Time Scale on the Device</strong></h2>



<p>Health data measured from wearable devices can reveal different information depending on the time scale at which it is analyzed. For example, short time segments can reveal rapidly changing signals such as heartbeats, while longer time segments can uncover patterns that accumulate over time, such as sleep or physical activity. This is similar to examining different information by zooming in and out of an image.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="680" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13152307/Samsung-Corporate-Biosignals-Health-Insights-Samsung-Research-Health-Foundation-Models_Main3.jpg" alt="Self-Supervised Learning From Wearable Data Across Multiple Time Scales" class="wp-image-176441" /></figure>



<p>HiMAE is a self-supervised learning model that learns representation from wearable data across multiple time scales. It uses multiple encoders to analyze short and long segments separately, allowing the model to identify information required for each health task, such as heart rate analysis or sleep prediction, at the appropriate time scale. During training, the model reconstructs masked parts of the data. This enables it to learn key patterns in biosignals even when labeled data is limited.</p>



<p>A single pretrained HiMAE model handles classification, numerical prediction and data generation. The model achieved high performance while being smaller than existing models. It also improved computational efficiency to the point where it can produce results in less than one millisecond on a smartwatch-class central processing unit (CPU). Most importantly, HiMAE demonstrates the potential of on-device health foundation models for the first time — analyzing raw health signals in real time, without relying on cloud servers.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>The Next Step Toward Connected Care</strong></h2>



<p>Together, xMAE and HiMAE, represent an advance in AI models that precisely understand the physiological relationships and temporal structures unique to biosignals. Both models aim to deliver precise, continuous, personalized health insights and to generalize across many health tasks from a single pretrained model. Hear more from the researchers behind this work below.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="620" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13153801/Samsung-Corporate-Biosignals-Health-Insights-Samsung-Research-Health-Foundation-Models_Main4.jpg" alt="Sharanya Desai" class="wp-image-176442" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="620" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13153812/Samsung-Corporate-Biosignals-Health-Insights-Samsung-Research-Health-Foundation-Models_Main5.jpg" alt="Subbu Venkatraman" class="wp-image-176443" /></figure>
]]></content:encoded>
																				</item>

																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics is bringing the world’s largest K-festival directly to homes worldwide. Starting August 14 through 16, Samsung TV Plus, the company’s subscription-free streaming TV service, will deliver live coverage of KCON LA 2026 across 17 countries, including France, India, Thailand, U.K., and the U.S.<sup data-fn="0b2aa461-3fe8-4019-82df-a506757bd342" class="fn"><a href="#0b2aa461-3fe8-4019-82df-a506757bd342" id="0b2aa461-3fe8-4019-82df-a506757bd342-link">1</a></sup></p>



<p>The three-day broadcast will stream performance from the TOYOTA MUSIC DEN STAGE and M COUNTDOWN STAGE through CJ ENM K-POP channel.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Bringing K-pop Home</strong></h2>



<p>Held at Crypto.com Arena and the Los Angeles Convention Center U.S., KCON LA 2026 continues to elevate the fan experience as the world’s largest K-Festival that’s led K-pop, K-culture and K-Lifestyle globalization since 2012, bringing together some of the biggest names from across the Korean entertainment landscape for unique performances that cannot be seen anywhere else.</p>



<p>For viewers unable to attend in person, Samsung TV Plus brings the energy and excitement of KCON LA 2026 directly to audiences through CJ ENM K-POP. The global K-pop performance channel on Samsung TV Plus features a curated lineup of performances, artist-led content and K-pop entertainment. Viewers across participating markets can watch KCON LA 2026 performances and festival programming at no additional cost. Local schedules and channel availability may vary by market.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="653" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12170043/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-TV-Plus-KCON-LA-2026_main1.jpg" alt="Broadcast times may vary by region due to time zone differences." class="wp-image-176421" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Broadcast times may vary by region due to time zone differences.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Creating a Concert Experience at Home</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung is enhancing the viewing experience through its connected ecosystem. Viewers can set a SmartThings reminder on a smartphone or Samsung TV to prepare for daily coverage. Those with compatible connected Samsung devices can pre-adjust lighting and sound settings to create an immersive concert atmosphere from home.</p>



<p>“Korean entertainment has become a defining force in global culture, and audiences are seeking more ways to experience the content they love,” said Gwiho Lee, Executive Vice President and Deputy Head of Service Business Team, Visual Display (VD) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Samsung TV Plus and CJ ENM are making this possible, bringing the energy of live K-pop and unforgettable performances from global K-pop stars to fans worldwide.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12170113/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-TV-Plus-KCON-LA-2026_main2-1.jpg" alt="Samsung TV Plus is available on Samsung TVs, Smart Monitors, Family Hub and Galaxy devices. Viewers can watch KCON LA 2026 free in 17 countries. (Australia, Brazil, Canada, France, Germany, India, Italy, Mexico, Netherlands, New Zealand, Philippines, Singapore, Spain, Sweden, Thailand, U.K., and U.S.)</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A Glowing Destination for Korean Content</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung TV Plus is available subscription-free on Samsung Smart TVs, Smart Monitor, Family Hub and Galaxy devices. The service offers more than 4,000 hours of Korean programming, from dramas and thrillers to romance series, crime programming, music, and live events.</p>



<p>In partnership with CJ ENM, a leading Korean entertainment company, Samsung TV Plus offers a range of dedicated CJ ENM channels, including CJ ENM K-POP, CJ ENM K-Stories, CJ ENM K-Reality, CJ ENM K-Food & Travel, and CJ ENM K-Novelas en Español ─ creating a comprehensive window into Korean culture through a single service.</p>



<p>Samsung TV Plus has more than 100 million monthly active users and carries nearly 5,000 ad-supported linear channels across 30 countries. Samsung TV Plus continues to bring Korean entertainment and live events to audiences worldwide.</p>

                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4woZNbR</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[London, a city of rich heritage and innovation, set the stage for Galaxy Unpacked July 2026, as Samsung Electronics unveiled its latest foldables — Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8. Combining refined hardware with powerful AI capabilities, the new Galaxy Z series delivers an advanced camera experience across professional-quality landscapes, […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>London, a city of rich heritage and innovation, set the stage for Galaxy Unpacked July 2026, as Samsung Electronics unveiled its latest foldables — Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8.</p>



<p>Combining refined hardware with powerful AI capabilities, the new Galaxy Z series delivers an advanced camera experience across professional-quality landscapes, dynamic video, and expressive selfies.</p>



<p>Fresh from Galaxy Unpacked July 2026, Samsung Newsroom took all three devices across London to put their cameras to the test.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra: Brilliant Detail, Day and Night</strong></h2>



<p>The journey began in Westminster. The Palace of Westminster, with Big Ben rising above it, sits along the River Thames, while the London Eye stands across the water on the South Bank — a view that brings together London’s past and present.</p>



<p>Shot with Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Big Ben and the surrounding cityscape came through in crisp detail.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12154811/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main1_F.jpg" alt="" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12154943/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main2.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ (From top to bottom) Photo taken with Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra and a cropped zoom of Big Ben enlarged by more than 10x</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra’s 200MP main camera brings distant subjects into sharp focus. A new high dynamic range (HDR) capability in 200MP mode delivers richer colors and finer detail. Zooming in reveals Big Ben’s intricate stonework and even the Latin inscription beneath the clock face, demonstrating Ultra-level clarity down to the smallest details.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12155426/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main3.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Photo taken with the 200MP camera from the London Eye on Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra</figcaption></figure>



<p>At 135 meters tall, the London Eye is difficult to fit into a single frame from up close. The Galaxy Z Fold series’ first 50MP ultra-wide camera makes it possible to capture the towering landmark, ticket office, and nearby visitors in a single shot.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12161054/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main4.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ (From left) Photos taken with the 1x camera and 50MP ultra-wide camera on Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra</figcaption></figure>



<p>After sunset, London took on a different character. At Tower Bridge, enhanced Nightography reduced noise in low light while preserving the illuminated bridge and texture of the flowing river in vivid detail.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12161607/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main5_F.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Tower Bridge captured with enhanced Nightography</figcaption></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12161722/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main6.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Photo taken with enhanced Nightography</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Fold8: Ready for Moments in Motion</strong></h2>



<p>Some travel moments are worth seeing from both sides of the camera. As Tower Bridge slowly lifted, Dual Recording captured the bridge in motion and the reactions of those watching in a single video.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12161854/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main7.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Dual Recording</figcaption></figure>



<p>Dual Recording uses the rear and front cameras simultaneously, with real-time previews on the expansive main display for easy framing. The two perspectives are combined into a single split-screen video, making the feature ideal for vlog-style content that captures both the moment and the reaction.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12162029/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main8.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Video recorded with Dual Recording</figcaption></figure>



<p>Away from the busy tourist hotspots, the journey continued to St. James’s Park. Filming one of the park’s resident pelicans proved challenging, as it moved quickly across the frame. For fast-moving action like this, Super Steady came in handy.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12162324/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main9.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Users can capture stable footage in high-motion situations.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Even as the phone moved sharply from side to side to follow the pelican, Super Steady with Horizontal Lock kept the footage level.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12162721/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main10.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Video recorded using Super Steady with Horizontal Lock</figcaption></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12163005/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main11.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Users can edit content on Galaxy Z Fold8’s large 7.6-inch main display.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8’s expansive 4:3 main display makes it easy to review clips, edit them to the desired aspect ratio, and share them with friends. From shooting to viewing and editing, the creative process flows seamlessly on one device.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Flip8: Freedom From Every Shot</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Z Flip8 brings more fun and flexibility to travel photography. With Flex Mode, users can position the phone for hands-free shots from multiple angles — without help from others.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12163234/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main12_F.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Flex Mode on Galaxy Z Flip8</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Z Flip8, the thinnest and lightest Galaxy Z Flip yet, fits comfortably in one hand. FlexWindow on the cover screen provides a real-time preview, making it easier to frame selfies.</p>



<p>Equipped with a 50MP wide-angle camera and AI-powered ProVisual Engine, Galaxy Z Flip8 captures high-quality selfies in fine detail, down to individual strands of hair.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12163702/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main13.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Galaxy Z Flip8’s improved AI Bokeh feature naturally separates subjects from the background for portraits with greater depth.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The journey ended aboard one of London’s iconic double-decker buses. Folded halfway, Galaxy Z Flip8’s Camcorder Grip made the device comfortable to hold like a camcorder while recording on the move.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12163821/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main14.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Camcorder Grip</figcaption></figure>



<p>Zoom Rocker with Camcorder Grip provides precise control over zoom speed for smooth, natural zoom-ins.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-create-block-wide-image"><img src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/12163850/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-London-with-Foldables_main15.jpg" alt="" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption test">▲ Camcorder Grip with Zoom Rocker</figcaption></figure>



<p>Exploring London with the latest Galaxy Z series revealed new ways to see — and remember — the city through each device.</p>



<p>With Galaxy cameras, Samsung will continue to help users capture the moments that matter and preserve those memories for years to come.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[[Video] Samsung AMS Transforms Shared Classroom Displays Into Personalized Workspaces for Teachers]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/video-samsung-ams-transforms-shared-classroom-displays-into-personalized-workspaces-for-teachers</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 12 Aug 2026 11:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11172631/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AMS-Shared-Classroom-Displays-Personal-Workspaces_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[TVs & Displays]]></category>

									<description><![CDATA[Shared interactive displays have become a central hub for classroom instruction, helping teachers access lesson materials and digital tools throughout the school day. However, when multiple teachers share the same display, keeping personal settings and classroom content private while ensuring a consistent experience can be challenging. Samsung AMS (Account Management Solution) helps transform shared Samsung […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Shared interactive displays have become a central hub for classroom instruction, helping teachers access lesson materials and digital tools throughout the school day. However, when multiple teachers share the same display, keeping personal settings and classroom content private while ensuring a consistent experience can be challenging.</p>



<p>Samsung AMS (Account Management Solution) helps transform shared Samsung Interactive Displays into personalized, secure workspaces that follow teachers across classrooms. With seamless sign-in and cloud-connected profiles, teachers can quickly access their files, apps, and settings wherever they teach.</p>



<p>Watch the video below to see how Samsung AMS helps educators access personalized workspaces while enabling schools to centrally manage accounts, connected displays, and emergency alerts.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio"><div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">
<iframe loading="lazy" title="Interactive Display: Make Shared Classrooms Feel Personal with AMS | Samsung" width="500" height="281" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/vSAs_e6DphQ?feature=oembed" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allowfullscreen></iframe>
</div></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A Personalized Workspace That Follows Teachers Across Classrooms</strong></h2>



<p>With Samsung AMS, each teacher can access a secure, cloud-connected workspace on compatible Samsung Interactive Displays.<sup data-fn="0fea2d43-0741-45d7-886e-155b3f64eb8e" class="fn"><a href="#0fea2d43-0741-45d7-886e-155b3f64eb8e" id="0fea2d43-0741-45d7-886e-155b3f64eb8e-link">1</a></sup> Instead of setting up their workspace every time they move to a different classroom, teachers can sign in with a QR code or NFC-enabled ID card<sup data-fn="008d98c7-ee7e-47e1-af4c-00d40011fbf1" class="fn"><a href="#008d98c7-ee7e-47e1-af4c-00d40011fbf1" id="008d98c7-ee7e-47e1-af4c-00d40011fbf1-link">2</a></sup> and immediately access their files, apps, and personalized settings, eliminating the need for password entry in the presence of students.  </p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11145556/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AMS-Shared-Classroom-Displays-Personal-Workspaces_Main1.jpg" alt="Samsung AMS lets each teacher access their own personalized workspace on a shared classroom display." class="wp-image-176310" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung AMS lets each teacher access their own personalized workspace on a shared classroom display.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Once signed in, teachers can access the same personalized workspace on any compatible Samsung Interactive Display. Their cloud-connected profile automatically loads their files, apps, wallpaper, bookmarks, and app shortcuts, creating a carefully arranged teaching environment.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11145626/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AMS-Shared-Classroom-Displays-Personal-Workspaces_Main2.jpg" alt="Jelena Živko, Senior Instructional Technology Specialist with Volusia County School District." class="wp-image-176311" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Jelena Živko, Senior Instructional Technology Specialist with Volusia County School District.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Jelena Živko, Senior Instructional Technology Specialist with Volusia County School District, highlighted how Samsung AMS helps teachers maintain a consistent experience as they move between classrooms.</p>



<p>“If I’m a math teacher and I have to teach in various classrooms, I can sign in, everything is ready to go, my lesson is there for me,” said Živko. “Being logged in with all of my credentials, I have access to all of my files, the tools, and my preferences. I can customize it to best fit my needs.”</p>



<p>In addition, Samsung AMS provides single sign-on access to the built-in Samsung AI Assistant app,<sup data-fn="c8569c51-3c5a-4409-9c12-bb1af9d92619" class="fn"><a href="#c8569c51-3c5a-4409-9c12-bb1af9d92619" id="c8569c51-3c5a-4409-9c12-bb1af9d92619-link">3</a></sup> allowing teachers to access previous lesson materials, including AI-generated summaries, without signing in again. With their workspace available across compatible displays, teachers can move between classrooms without losing access to the tools and lesson materials they use every day.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="321" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11145739/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AMS-Shared-Classroom-Displays-Personal-Workspaces_Main3.jpg" alt="Teachers can access personalized app shortcuts, bookmarks and lesson materials on any compatible Samsung Interactive Display." class="wp-image-176312" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Teachers can access personalized app shortcuts, bookmarks and lesson materials on any compatible Samsung Interactive Display.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Because classroom displays are often shared by multiple teachers, Samsung AMS helps protect each teacher’s workspace. Each teacher receives a secure and encrypted workspace, helping keep personal settings and classroom content protected from unauthorized access.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Simplifying School IT Management With Samsung Education Portal</strong></h2>



<p>As schools deploy more shared classroom displays, they need a simple way to manage teacher accounts, connected displays, and Emergency Alerts.</p>



<p>Samsung Education Portal centralizes these tasks, allowing IT teams to register teachers, enroll compatible Samsung Interactive Displays, and link NFC-enabled ID cards to user accounts from a single portal.<sup data-fn="09d361c3-32a9-4d5d-98e3-8b8e60b7c3ea" class="fn"><a href="#09d361c3-32a9-4d5d-98e3-8b8e60b7c3ea" id="09d361c3-32a9-4d5d-98e3-8b8e60b7c3ea-link">4</a></sup> Displays can also be organized using location tags by school, building, or classroom, making large deployments easier to manage.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="321" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11145813/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AMS-Shared-Classroom-Displays-Personal-Workspaces_Main4.jpg" alt="Samsung Education Portal gives IT teams a centralized way to manage teacher accounts, enrolled displays and Emergency Alerts." class="wp-image-176313" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung Education Portal gives IT teams a centralized way to manage teacher accounts, enrolled displays and Emergency Alerts.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung Education Portal also simplifies Emergency Alert management by integrating with major notification platforms, including InformaCast and Raptor.<sup data-fn="2f1730d2-bb47-4773-b304-9a80909b558f" class="fn"><a href="#2f1730d2-bb47-4773-b304-9a80909b558f" id="2f1730d2-bb47-4773-b304-9a80909b558f-link">5</a></sup> IT teams can configure custom alert templates in advance and assign them to selected displays or display groups.</p>



<p>When an emergency signal is received, the appropriate alert automatically appears on enrolled Samsung Interactive Displays. This gives schools a visual way to share urgent information across displays throughout the building.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11145900/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AMS-Shared-Classroom-Displays-Personal-Workspaces_Main5.gif" alt="Emergency Alerts are instantly displayed across selected Samsung Interactive Displays throughout a school." class="wp-image-176314" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Emergency Alerts are instantly displayed across selected Samsung Interactive Displays throughout a school.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Leah Fuller, Sr. Manager Vertical Sales Leader from Best Buy Education, highlighted the impact of Samsung Education Portal’s Emergency Alert feature. “I think that gives a ton of assurance not just to staff but students and their families as well,” said Fuller.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11145939/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AMS-Shared-Classroom-Displays-Personal-Workspaces_Main6.jpg" alt="Leah Fuller, Sr. Manager Vertical Sales Leader from Best Buy Education." class="wp-image-176315" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Leah Fuller, Sr. Manager Vertical Sales Leader from Best Buy Education.</figcaption></figure>



<p>By creating personalized classroom experiences for teachers and simplifying how shared displays are managed, Samsung AMS helps schools create more seamless and secure learning environments.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics has introduced a new ringtone featuring a classical masterpiece on the Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8. Performed by pianist Yekwon Sunwoo, Frédéric Chopin’s “Nocturne in E-flat Major, Op. 9, No. 2” adds a lyrical dimension to everyday life. Sunwoo became the first Korean to win the Van […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics has introduced a new ringtone featuring a classical masterpiece on the Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8.</p>



<p>Performed by pianist Yekwon Sunwoo, Frédéric Chopin’s “Nocturne in E-flat Major, Op. 9, No. 2” adds a lyrical dimension to everyday life. Sunwoo became the first Korean to win the Van Cliburn International Piano Competition in 2017 and holds the record for the most international competition wins by a Korean pianist. More than 190 years after Chopin composed the piece, Sunwoo offers a fresh interpretation that can now be heard on the latest Galaxy devices.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio"><div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">
<iframe loading="lazy" title="피아니스트 선우예권의 클래식 벨소리 녹음 메이킹 (Making of Pianist Yekwon Sunwoo’s Classical Ringtone Recording Sessions)" width="500" height="281" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/oobjX4v15Hg?feature=oembed" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allowfullscreen></iframe>
</div><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Behind-the-scenes footage of the Chopin nocturne ringtone recording</figcaption></figure>



<p><a id="_msocom_1"></a></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Chopin’s Nocturne for One UI 9</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung’s choice of Chopin’s “Nocturne in E-flat Major, Op. 9, No. 2” as a new ringtone carries special significance. To celebrate the release of One UI 9, Samsung selected the piece from Chopin’s Op. 9 — a fitting nod to the shared number. Long cherished worldwide for its serene melody and delicate emotional expression, the nocturne lends itself naturally to alarms that start the day and ringtones that signal incoming calls.</p>



<p>The new nocturne ringtone debuts with One UI 9 on Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8.</p>



<p></p>



<figure class="wp-block-audio"><audio controls src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11162243/Chopin_Nocturne_Op_9_No_2.wav"></audio></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11162740/Samsung-Mobile-New-Ringtone-Chopin-Nocturne_main1.jpg" alt="Performed by pianist Yekwon Sunwoo, Frédéric Chopin’s “Nocturne in E-flat Major, Op. 9, No. 2” adds a lyrical dimension to everyday life. " class="wp-image-176338" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Yekwon Sunwoo</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Capturing Chopin for Galaxy</strong></h2>



<p>The recording took place at Samsung Galaxy Sound Studios, located at Samsung Seoul R&D Campus — the very space where Galaxy’s sound identity is shaped.</p>



<p>Byeong Joon Hwang and Young Jae Jang of Sound Mirror Korea — who have collaborated with Samsung on several projects — oversaw the recording and mixing, carefully preserving the nuances of the performance. Master piano technician Hyungu Kang refined the instrument’s tone, ensuring clarity and precision in every note.</p>



<p>“The warm piano tone and the acoustics of the studio made it an ideal setting for performing the nocturne,” said Sunwoo. “It was meaningful to bring the comfort and timeless emotion of classical music to Galaxy devices used by people around the world.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/11162817/Samsung-Mobile-New-Ringtone-Chopin-Nocturne_main2.jpg" alt="The recording took place at Samsung Galaxy Sound Studios, located at Samsung Seoul R&D Campus — the very space where Galaxy’s sound identity is shaped." class="wp-image-176339" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Sunwoo performs Chopin’s nocturne.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A Classical Touch to Everyday Life</strong></h2>



<p>More than a simple alert, the new ringtone carries the lingering emotion of classical music into the devices people turn to throughout the day. This connection reflects Galaxy’s philosophy of delivering sound experiences that extend beyond the device itself.</p>



<p>“The nocturne evokes the stillness of the night, and we wanted its gentle resonance to weave naturally into the rhythms of the day,” said Myoung Woo Nam, Principal Sound Designer, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “We hope that encountering classical music in everyday life will create special moments for users.”</p>



<p>Samsung will continue to apply a consistent sound philosophy across its products to enhance the user experience and further expand Galaxy’s distinctive sound identity.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung’s Hearing Aid Feature on Galaxy Buds Cleared by FDA]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsungs-hearing-aid-feature-on-galaxy-buds-cleared-by-fda</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 11 Aug 2026 22:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/10105607/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Hearing-Aid-Feature-Galaxy-Buds-FDA_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[FDA]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Buds3 Pro]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Buds4 Pro]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[hearing aid]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4xrKIat</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that its Galaxy Buds Hearing Aid feature has received U.S. Food and Drug Administration (FDA) clearance. The feature enables compatible Galaxy Buds to serve as an over-the-counter (OTC) hearing aid, offering users 18 years and older a convenient solution for addressing perceived mild to moderate hearing loss — which may be […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that its Galaxy Buds <strong>Hearing Aid</strong> feature has received U.S. Food and Drug Administration (FDA) clearance. The feature enables compatible Galaxy Buds<sup data-fn="4a0959d9-20d3-47a9-a213-c7c7df560085" class="fn"><a href="#4a0959d9-20d3-47a9-a213-c7c7df560085" id="4a0959d9-20d3-47a9-a213-c7c7df560085-link">1</a></sup> to serve as an over-the-counter (OTC) hearing aid,<sup data-fn="ab550802-908c-4d66-98af-9cf8a96f4b60" class="fn"><a href="#ab550802-908c-4d66-98af-9cf8a96f4b60" id="ab550802-908c-4d66-98af-9cf8a96f4b60-link">2</a></sup> offering users 18 years and older a convenient solution for addressing perceived mild to moderate hearing loss — which may be indicated by difficulty following conversations in noisy environments — without a prescription or clinic visit. Users can also diagnose their hearing with the Galaxy Buds <strong>Hearing Test</strong> feature, which is registered as a Software as a Medical Device (SaMD) and provides medical-grade results.</p>



<p>“Hearing health is a vital component of our overall well-being, profoundly impacting how we communicate, connect with loved ones and engage with the world,” said Hon Pak, Senior Vice President and Head of the Digital Health Team for the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “With the latest clearance from the FDA, we are expanding access to hearing support and making proactive care effortless by integrating the Hearing Test and Hearing Aid functionality directly into the Galaxy Buds.”</p>



<p>Hearing loss is incredibly common, yet it remains one of the most overlooked health conditions worldwide. The World Health Organization (WHO) estimates that globally, more than 1.5 billion people experience some degree of hearing loss — a figure projected to reach 2.5 billion people by 2050. Despite this, many people who could benefit from hearing aids do not use them due to high cost, social stigma or limited access to professional care.</p>



<p>Samsung has been committed to advancing hearing support technologies for consumers since 2020. That year, the company introduced a Personal Sound Amplification Product (PSAP) feature on Galaxy Buds+.</p>



<p>The PSAP feature was clinically validated in 2021 as effective support for daily communication for those with hearing loss, building the foundation for the Hearing Aid feature. To develop this new OTC solution, Samsung collaborated closely with the National Acoustic Laboratories (NAL) and Samsung Medical Center (SMC). The resulting technology is backed by robust clinical validation studies conducted at Vanderbilt University Medical Center, San Jose State University and the University of Memphis.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/10105731/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Hearing-Aid-Feature-Galaxy-Buds-FDA_main1.jpg" alt="By navigating to the Galaxy Buds menu in Settings, users can complete a self-administered Hearing Test in just five minutes." class="wp-image-176281" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Example of a Hearing Test result</figcaption></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/10105810/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Hearing-Aid-Feature-Galaxy-Buds-FDA_main2.jpg" alt="Based on those results, the Hearing Aid feature amplifies the sounds users have difficulty hearing." class="wp-image-176282" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Hearing Aid settings screen</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung’s holistic hearing health experience centers around two major features integrated directly into Galaxy Buds without requiring a separate app.<sup data-fn="2bc4773d-223c-4d41-8a28-28641e6d5d38" class="fn"><a href="#2bc4773d-223c-4d41-8a28-28641e6d5d38" id="2bc4773d-223c-4d41-8a28-28641e6d5d38-link">3</a></sup> By navigating to the Galaxy Buds menu in Settings, users can complete a self-administered <strong>Hearing Test</strong> in just five minutes. Using clinical-grade, pure-tone audiometry, the software tests each ear individually to pinpoint the user’s specific hearing deficits. It then generates an audiogram with accuracy comparable to a professional audiologist’s evaluation. The results categorize the user’s hearing as normal or one of four stages of hearing loss: mild, moderate, severe or profound.</p>



<p>Based on those results, the <strong>Hearing Aid </strong>feature amplifies the sounds users have difficulty hearing — such as high frequencies, surrounding whispers and distant voices — personalized to each user’s hearing profile. Hearing Aid also supports additional advanced features typically found in premium hearing aids, such as noise elimination and beamforming, which help focus sounds in front of the user and suppress background noise.</p>



<p>Galaxy Buds use NAL-NL2, recognized globally as the clinical standard formula for fitting prescription hearing aid. This advanced approach ensures highly personalized, accurate results tailored to each individual’s hearing profile.</p>



<p>To give users a comprehensive view of their overall wellness, hearing-related insights — including noise exposure levels and Hearing Test results — are accessible directly within the Samsung Health app.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong></h2>



<p>The Hearing Aid and Hearing Test features will be available on Galaxy Buds3 Pro and Galaxy Buds4 Pro in the U.S. and select approved markets in Q4 2026.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="4a0959d9-20d3-47a9-a213-c7c7df560085">Available on Galaxy Buds3 Pro and Galaxy Buds4 Pro only. <a href="#4a0959d9-20d3-47a9-a213-c7c7df560085-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="ab550802-908c-4d66-98af-9cf8a96f4b60">Availability may vary by market, model or paired device. The Hearing Aid feature is intended for users 18 years and older with perceived mild to moderate hearing loss and is not a substitute for professional diagnosis or treatment by a qualified clinician. <a href="#ab550802-908c-4d66-98af-9cf8a96f4b60-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="2bc4773d-223c-4d41-8a28-28641e6d5d38">Full support requires a compatible Galaxy device with One UI 9 or later. <a href="#2bc4773d-223c-4d41-8a28-28641e6d5d38-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 3"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>
